    <i>gaṅgeśa</i> [<a href="#entry-iiza" class="skt-link">īśa</a>] <a href="#entry-gafgeza" class="skt-link">m.</a> hist. np. de Gaṅgeśa ou Gaṅgeśopādhyāya «Maître du Gange», philosophe de la fin du 13⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ siècle originaire du Bengale; il enseignait à <a href="#entry-Umithilaa" class="skt-link">Mithilā</a>; il investigua les notions de vérité et d'inférence logique [<a href="#entry-praamaa_nya" class="skt-link">prāmāṇya</a>]; il est l'auteur du <a href="#entry-Utattvacintaama_ni" class="skt-link">Tattvacintāmaṇi</a>, et ainsi fondateur [<a href="#entry-muulagranthakaara" class="skt-link">mūlagranthakāra</a>] de l'école de sémiotique ou nouvelle logique [<a href="#entry-navyanyaaya" class="skt-link">navyanyāya</a>] de <a href="#entry-Umithilaa" class="skt-link">Mithilā</a>; <a href="#entry-Uvardhamaana_1" class="skt-link">Vardhamāna_1</a> est son fils.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-gafgezvara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>gaṅgeśvara</i> [<a href="#entry-iizvara" class="skt-link">īśvara</a>] <a href="#entry-gafgezvara" class="skt-link">m.</a> hist. np. du roi Gaṅgeśvara du <a href="#entry-Ukalifga" class="skt-link">Kaliṅga</a>; cf. <a href="#entry-Uanantavarmaa" class="skt-link">Anantavarmā</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-gafgodaka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>gaṅgodaka</i> [<a href="#entry-udaka" class="skt-link">udaka</a>] <a href="#entry-gafgodaka" class="skt-link">n.</a> eau du Gange.<br> <a href="#entry-gafgaa" class="skt-link">gaṅgā</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-gafgotrii">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>gaṅgotrī</i> [<a href="#entry-utt_rr" class="skt-link">uttṝ</a>] <a href="#entry-gafgotrii" class="skt-link">f.</a> géo. np. de Gaṅgotrī, lieu de pélerinage et glacier au nord de l'Uttar Pradesh, d'où est issu le Gange; cf. <a href="#entry-Ugomukha" class="skt-link">Gomukha</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-gagana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">गगन</span> <i>gagana</i> <a href="#entry-gagana" class="skt-link">n.</a> ciel.<br> <i>gaganakusumāni</i> <i>ci</i> bâtir des châteaux en Espagne.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-gahana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">गहन</span> <i>gahana</i> [<a href="#entry-gaah" class="skt-link">gāh</a><a href="#entry--ana" class="skt-link">-ana</a>] a. <a href="#entry-gahana" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-gahana" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-gahanaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>gahanā</i> épais, profond; impénétrable; inexplicable — <a href="#entry-gahana" class="skt-link">n.</a> cachette, fourré épais; endroit inaccessible.<br> <i>gahanīkṛ</i> rendre inaccessible.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-gahanatva">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>gahanatva</i> [<a href="#entry--tva" class="skt-link">-tva</a>] <a href="#entry-gahanatva" class="skt-link">n.</a> inaccessibilité.<br> <a href="#entry-gahana" class="skt-link">gahana</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-gai">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">गै</span> <i>gai</i> cf. <a href="#entry-gaa_2" class="skt-link">gā_2</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-gaj">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">गज्</span> <i>gaj</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. <i>(gajati)</i> pft. <i>(jagāja)</i> mugir, barrir.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-gaja">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">गज</span> <i>gaja</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-gaj" class="skt-link">gaj</a>] <a href="#entry-gaja" class="skt-link">m.</a> éléphant <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> math. symb. le nombre 8 — <a href="#entry-gajii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>gajī</i> éléphant femelle.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-gajaanana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>gajānana</i> [<a href="#entry-aanana" class="skt-link">ānana</a>] <a href="#entry-gajaanana" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. de Gajānana, épith. de <a href="#entry-Uga_neza" class="skt-link">Gaṇeśa</a> «à la tête d'éléphant».
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-gajaasura">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>gajāsura</i> [<a href="#entry-asura" class="skt-link">asura</a>] <a href="#entry-gajaasura" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. démon-éléphant <a href="#entry-Ugaya_1" class="skt-link">Gaya_1</a>, tué par <a href="#entry-Uziva" class="skt-link">Śiva</a>; cf. <a href="#entry-Udaarukavana" class="skt-link">Dārukavana</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. démon-éléphant <a href="#entry-Uniila" class="skt-link">Nīla</a>, tué par <a href="#entry-Uga_neza" class="skt-link">Gaṇeśa</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-gajaasurasa_mhaara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>gajāsurasaṃhāra</i> [<a href="#entry-sa_mhaara" class="skt-link">saṃhāra</a>] <a href="#entry-gajaasurasa_mhaara" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. destruction du démon-éléphant par <a href="#entry-Uziva" class="skt-link">Śiva</a>; en iconographie, on représente <a href="#entry-Uziva" class="skt-link">Śiva</a> revêtu de sa peau.<br> <a href="#entry-gajaasura" class="skt-link">gajāsura</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-gajadaghna">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>gajadaghna</i> [<a href="#entry-daghna" class="skt-link">daghna</a>] a. <a href="#entry-gajadaghna" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-gajadaghna" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-gajadaghnaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>gajadaghnā</i> <a href="#entry-gajadaghnii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>gajadaghnī</i> qui a (de l'eau) jusqu'à hauteur d'un éléphant.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-gajagaamin">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>gajagāmin</i> [<a href="#entry-gaamin" class="skt-link">gāmin</a>] a. <a href="#entry-gajagaamin" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-gajagaamin" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-gajagaaminii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>gajagāminī</i> qui a la démarche (majestueuse) de l'éléphant.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-gajagati">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>gajagati</i> [<a href="#entry-gati" class="skt-link">gati</a>] <a href="#entry-gajagati" class="skt-link">f.</a> phon. nom d'un mètre de 4 pieds de 8 syllabes de motif <i>nabhalagā</i>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-gajagraha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>gajagraha</i> [<a href="#entry-graha" class="skt-link">graha</a>] <a href="#entry-gajagraha" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. [BhP.] np. du crocodile [<a href="#entry-makara" class="skt-link">makara</a>] Gajagraha, qui s'attaqua à l'éléphant <a href="#entry-Ugajendra" class="skt-link">Gajendra</a>; il était le roi <a href="#entry-gandharva" class="skt-link">gandharva</a> <a href="#entry-Uhuhu" class="skt-link">Huhu</a>, maudit par le sage <a href="#entry-Udevala" class="skt-link">Devala</a> pour lui avoir tiré la jambe alors qu'il invoquait <a href="#entry-Usuurya" class="skt-link">Sūrya</a> lors de son bain sacré; il fut libéré de sa malédiction en étant décapité par le <a href="#entry-cakra" class="skt-link">cakra</a> de <a href="#entry-Uvi_s_nu" class="skt-link">Viṣṇu</a> invoqué par <a href="#entry-Ugajendra" class="skt-link">Gajendra</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-gajalak_smii">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>gajalakṣmī</i> [<a href="#entry-lak_smii" class="skt-link">lakṣmī</a>] <a href="#entry-gajalak_smii" class="skt-link">f.</a> myth. «<a href="#entry-Ulak_smii" class="skt-link">Lakṣmī</a> aux éléphants», représentation iconographique de la lustration de <a href="#entry-Ulak_smii" class="skt-link">Lakṣmī</a> par deux éléphants, représentant les trésors de <a href="#entry-Ukubera" class="skt-link">Kubera</a> <a href="#entry-Upadmanidhi" class="skt-link">Padmanidhi</a> et <a href="#entry-Uzafkhanidhi" class="skt-link">Śaṅkhanidhi</a>; elle se trouve en gén. au fronton du seuil d'accès au sanctuaire principal [<a href="#entry-garbhag_rha" class="skt-link">garbhagṛha</a>] d'un temple.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-gajamedha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>gajamedha</i> [<a href="#entry-medha" class="skt-link">medha</a>] <a href="#entry-gajamedha" class="skt-link">m.</a> soc. sacrifice d'éléphant.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-gajamela">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>gajamela</i> [<a href="#entry-mela" class="skt-link">mela</a>] <a href="#entry-gajamela" class="skt-link">m.</a> soc. rassemblement des éléphants, une fête du <a href="#entry-Ukerala" class="skt-link">Kerala</a>, en janvier, not. à Kochi.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-gajamukha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>gajamukha</i> [<a href="#entry-mukha" class="skt-link">mukha</a>] a. <a href="#entry-gajamukha" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-gajamukha" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-gajamukhii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>gajamukhī</i> qui a une tête d'éléphant — <a href="#entry-gajamukha" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. de Gajamukha, démon [<a href="#entry-asura" class="skt-link">asura</a>] à tête d'éléphant; par ses austérités, il obtint un vœu de <a href="#entry-Uziva" class="skt-link">Śiva</a>, et demanda à celui-ci de vivre dans son ventre; <a href="#entry-Uvi_s_nu" class="skt-link">Viṣṇu</a> l'en délivra.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-gajap_r_s_thak_rti">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>gajapṛṣṭhakṛti</i> [<a href="#entry-p_r_s_tha" class="skt-link">pṛṣṭha</a>-<a href="#entry-k_rti" class="skt-link">kṛti</a>] <a href="#entry-gajap_r_s_thak_rti" class="skt-link">f.</a> arch. abside «en dos d'éléphant».
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-gajapati">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>gajapati</i> [<a href="#entry-pati" class="skt-link">pati</a>] <a href="#entry-gajapati" class="skt-link">m.</a> hist. np. de Gajapati «Maître des éléphants», titre dynastique des rois de <a href="#entry-Uka_taka" class="skt-link">Kaṭaka</a>, au <a href="#entry-Ukalifga" class="skt-link">Kaliṅga</a>; cf. <a href="#entry-Uprataaparudradeva" class="skt-link">Pratāparudradeva</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. [BhP.] l'un des 4 éléphants cosmiques établis par <a href="#entry-Ubrahmaa" class="skt-link">Brahmā</a> pour maintenir l'équilibre du monde: <a href="#entry-U_r_sabha_1" class="skt-link">Ṛṣabha_1</a>, <a href="#entry-Upu_skaracuu_da" class="skt-link">Puṣkaracūḍa</a>, <a href="#entry-Uvaamana_1" class="skt-link">Vāmana_1</a> et <a href="#entry-Uaparaajita_1" class="skt-link">Aparājita_1</a>; ils se tiennent sur la montagne <a href="#entry-Ulokaaloka" class="skt-link">Lokāloka</a> délimitant le monde.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-gajapippalii">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>gajapippalī</i> [<a href="#entry-pippalii" class="skt-link">pippalī</a>] <a href="#entry-gajapippalii" class="skt-link">f.</a> natu. bot. <i>Scindapsus officinalis</i>, philodendron, liane grimpant le long des troncs d'arbre; ses fruits, parfumés, sont utilisés en médecine; syn. <a href="#entry-utkataa" class="skt-link">utkatā</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-gajasi_mha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>gajasiṃha</i> [<a href="#entry-si_mha" class="skt-link">siṃha</a>] <a href="#entry-gajasi_mha" class="skt-link">m.</a> arch. motif du lion terrassant l'éléphant, symbole de la victoire de l'hindouisme sur le bouddhisme.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-gajasuutra">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>gajasūtra</i> [<a href="#entry-suutra" class="skt-link">sūtra</a>] <a href="#entry-gajasuutra" class="skt-link">n.</a> gram. np. du Gajasūtra «Règle de l'éléphant», np. du <a href="#entry-suutra" class="skt-link">sūtra</a> 1 . 3 . 67 de l'<a href="#entry-Ua_s_taadhyaayii" class="skt-link">Aṣṭādhyāyī</a>, qui prescrit le moyen du causatif quand l'objet du verbe devient l'agent au causatif.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-gajataa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>gajatā</i> [<a href="#entry--taa" class="skt-link">-tā</a>] <a href="#entry-gajataa" class="skt-link">f.</a> condition éléphantesque <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> multitude d'éléphants.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-gajazaastra">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>gajaśāstra</i> [<a href="#entry-zaastra" class="skt-link">śāstra</a>] <a href="#entry-gajazaastra" class="skt-link">n.</a> lit. science (vétérinaire) des éléphants.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-gajendra">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>gajendra</i> [<a href="#entry-indra" class="skt-link">indra</a>] <a href="#entry-gajendra" class="skt-link">m.</a> chef des éléphants <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. [BhP.] np. de Gajendra, roi des éléphants de la forêt <a href="#entry-U_rtumat" class="skt-link">Ṛtumat</a>; parti se baigner au lac, il fut saisi à la jambe par le crocodile [<a href="#entry-makara" class="skt-link">makara</a>] <a href="#entry-Ugajagraha" class="skt-link">Gajagraha</a>; incapable de s'en libérer, il implora <a href="#entry-Uvi_s_nu" class="skt-link">Viṣṇu</a>, qui le libéra et lui apprit qu'il était dans une vie antérieure <a href="#entry-Uindradyumna" class="skt-link">Indradyumna</a>, maudit pour avoir manqué de respect à <a href="#entry-Uagastya" class="skt-link">Agastya</a>; il symbolise l'homme aveuglé par son orgueil et libéré par la foi [<a href="#entry-bhakti" class="skt-link">bhakti</a>].
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-gajendramok_sa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>gajendramokṣa</i> [<a href="#entry-mok_sa" class="skt-link">mokṣa</a>] <a href="#entry-gajendramok_sa" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. [BhP.] libération de l'éléphant <a href="#entry-Ugajendra" class="skt-link">Gajendra</a>, pris au piège et menacé par un crocodile; il pria <a href="#entry-Uvi_s_nu" class="skt-link">Viṣṇu</a> pour son salut, et à son arrivée, lui donna le lotus qu'il avait cueilli avec sa trompe; il symbolise la libération du dévot par la prière; cf. <a href="#entry-Uhariharanaatha" class="skt-link">Hariharanātha</a> — <a href="#entry-gajendramok_sa" class="skt-link">n.</a> [<i>gajendramokṣastotra</i>] cf. <a href="#entry-gajendrastuti" class="skt-link">gajendrastuti</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-gajendramok_sa_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>gajendramokṣaṇa</i> [<a href="#entry-mok_sa_na" class="skt-link">mokṣaṇa</a>] <a href="#entry-gajendramok_sa_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> lit. [<a href="#entry-Umahaabhaarata" class="skt-link">Mah.</a>] np. du Gajendramokṣaṇa, épisode de la libération de <a href="#entry-Ugajendra" class="skt-link">Gajendra</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-gajendrastuti">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>gajendrastuti</i> [<a href="#entry-stuti" class="skt-link">stuti</a>] <a href="#entry-gajendrastuti" class="skt-link">f.</a> lit. hymne à la gloire de <a href="#entry-Uvi_s_nu" class="skt-link">Viṣṇu</a>, invoqué par <a href="#entry-Ugajendra" class="skt-link">Gajendra</a> prisonnier; aussi appelé <i>gajendramokṣastotra</i>; c'est le premier <a href="#entry-zloka" class="skt-link">śloka</a> du <a href="#entry-Uvi_s_nusahasranaamastotra" class="skt-link">Viṣṇusahasranāmastotra</a>.<br> <i>śuklāṃbaradharaṃ</i> <i>viṣṇuṃ</i> <i>śaśivarṇaṃ</i> <i>caturbhujam</i> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> <i>prasannavadanaṃ</i> <i>dhyāyeta</i> <i>sarvavighnopaśāntaye</i> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> [<a href="#entry-gajendrastuti" class="skt-link">gajendrastuti</a>] Soyez contemplé, Ô <a href="#entry-Uvi_s_nu" class="skt-link">Viṣṇu</a> aux 4 bras, au teint de lune, vêtu de blanc, au visage serein, afin que cessent tous les obstacles.<br> <a href="#entry-gajendra" class="skt-link">gajendra</a> <br> <a href="#entry-gaja" class="skt-link">gaja</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-gakaara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">गकार</span> <i>gakāra</i> [<i>(ga)</i>-<a href="#entry-kaara" class="skt-link">kāra</a>] <a href="#entry-gakaara" class="skt-link">m.</a> le son ou la lettre ‘<i>ga</i>’.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-gal">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">गल्</span> <i>gal</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. <i>(galati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>galita</i><i>)</i> pf. <i>(</i><i>vi</i><i>)</i> couler, goutter, fuir, s'échapper; dégoutter, s'écouler <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> s'évanouir, disparaître <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> avaler — ca. <i>(gālayati)</i> égoutter, filtrer, presser — int. <i>(jalgalīti)</i> avaler répétitivement, lapper.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-gala">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">गल</span> <i>gala</i> [<a href="#entry-gal" class="skt-link">gal</a>] <a href="#entry-gala" class="skt-link">m.</a> [«qui avale»] gorge, cou.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-galbh">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">गल्भ्</span> <i>galbh</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. md. <i>(galbhate)</i> pf. <i>(</i><i>pra</i><i>)</i> être résolu; être hardi.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-galita">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">गलित</span> <i>galita</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-gal" class="skt-link">gal</a>] a. <a href="#entry-galita" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-galita" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-galitaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>galitā</i> qui s'égoutte <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> tombé, écoulé; épuisé, affaibli; dégradé, pourri; perdu.<br> <i>aṅgaṃ</i> <i>galitaṃ</i> <i>palitaṃ</i> <i>muṇḍaṃ</i> <i>daśanavihīnaṃ</i> <i>jātaṃ</i> <i>tuṇḍam</i> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> <i>vṛddho</i> <i>gṛhītvā</i> <i>daṇḍaṃ</i> <i>tadapi</i> <i>na</i> <i>muñcati</i> <i>āśā</i> <i>piṇḍam</i> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> [<a href="#entry-Uzafkaraacaarya" class="skt-link">Śaṅkarācārya</a>] Le corps s'est affaibli, les cheveux sont gris, les dents sont tombées, le vieillard marche avec un bâton, et pourtant il ne renonce pas à ses désirs.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-galitaku_s_tha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>galitakuṣṭha</i> [<a href="#entry-ku_s_tha" class="skt-link">kuṣṭha</a>] <a href="#entry-galitaku_s_tha" class="skt-link">n.</a> méd. lèpre avancée, où les doigts et les orteils tombent.<br> <a href="#entry-galita" class="skt-link">galita</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-gam">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">गम्</span> <i>gam</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. <i>(gacchati)</i> fut. <i>(</i><i>gamiṣyati</i><i>)</i> imp. <i>(gaccha)</i> pft. <i>(jagāma)</i> aor. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] <i>(agan)</i> aor. [<span class="skt-grammar">2</span>] <i>(agamat)</i> aor. [<span class="skt-grammar">3</span>] <i>(ajīgamat)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>gata</i><i>)</i> ppr. <i>(gacchat)</i> ppft. <i>(jagmivas)</i> pfp. <i>(</i><i>gantavya</i>, <i>gamya</i>, <i>gamanīya</i><i>)</i> abs. <i>(</i><i>gatvā</i>, <i>-gamya</i><i>)</i> inf. <i>(gantum)</i> pf. <i>(</i><i>ati</i>, <i>adhi</i>, <i>anu</i>, <i>abhi</i>, <i>apa</i>, <i>api</i>, <i>ava</i>, <i>ā</i>, <i>ut</i>, <i>upa</i>, <i>ni</i>, <i>nis</i>, <i>parā</i>, <i>pra</i>, <i>prati</i>, <i>vi</i>, <i>sam</i><i>)</i> aller, se mouvoir, marcher; aller dans <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> aller vers, atteindre; devenir, tomber dans ⟨acc.⟩; obtenir; encourir <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> partir, s'en aller, disparaître — ca. <i>(gamayati)</i> faire aller, mettre en mouvement, conduire, envoyer <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> passer (le temps) — ps. <i>(gamyate)</i> être allé, être parti; être compris — dés. <i>(jigamiṣati)</i> vouloir aller — int. md. <i>(jaṅgamyate)</i> marcher de manière courbe <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> lat. <i>venio</i>; all. kommen; ang. to come; fr. venir.<br> <i>astaṃ</i> <i>gam</i> disparaître derrière l'horizon; se coucher (astre).<br> <i>pṛthivīṃ</i> <i>gam</i> se prosterner.<br> <i>bhaikṣaṃ</i> <i>gam</i> demander l'aumône; mendier.<br> <i>mohaṃ</i> <i>gam</i> s'évanouir.<br> <i>kale</i> <i>gacchati</i> adv. le temps venu.<br> <i>asato</i> <i>mā</i> <i>sad</i> <i>gamaya</i> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> <i>tamaso</i> <i>mā</i> <i>jyotir</i> <i>gamaya</i> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> <i>mṛtyor</i> <i>mā'mṛtaṃ</i> <i>gamaya</i> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> [BU.] Mène-nous du néant à l'Être; mène-nous de l'obscurité à la Lumière; mène-nous de la mort à l'Éternité.<br> <i>tamaso</i> <i>mā</i> <i>jyotir</i> <i>gamaya</i> (devise de l'IIT de Kanpur) Mène-nous de l'ignorance au savoir.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-gama">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">गम</span> <i>gama</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-gam" class="skt-link">gam</a>] ifc. a. <a href="#entry-gama" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-gama" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-gamaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>gamā</i> qui va; ayant pour premier, précédé de.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-gamana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">गमन</span> <i>gamana</i> [act. <a href="#entry-gam" class="skt-link">gam</a>] <a href="#entry-gamana" class="skt-link">n.</a> [«fait d'aller»] marche, démarche; départ; déplacement, voyage <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> phil. [<a href="#entry-vaize_sika" class="skt-link">vaiśeṣika</a>] l'activité [<a href="#entry-karman" class="skt-link">karman</a>] de déplacement — ifc. (agt. ⟨g.⟩) allant à ⟨iic.⟩ — a. <a href="#entry-gamana" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-gamana" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-gamanaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>gamanā</i> ifc. qui a des rapports sexuels avec ⟨iic.⟩.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-gamanaagamana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>gamanāgamana</i> [<a href="#entry-aagamana" class="skt-link">āgamana</a>] <a href="#entry-gamanaagamana" class="skt-link">n.</a> aller-retour; va et vient.<br> <a href="#entry-gamana" class="skt-link">gamana</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-gamaniiya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">गमनीय</span> <i>gamanīya</i> [pfp. [<span class="skt-grammar">2</span>] <a href="#entry-gam" class="skt-link">gam</a>] a. <a href="#entry-gamaniiya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-gamaniiya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-gamaniiyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>gamanīyā</i> où l'on peut ou doit aller; accessible — ifc. qui consiste en, relatif à.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-gambhaarii">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">गम्भारी</span> <i>gambhārī</i> <a href="#entry-gambhaarii" class="skt-link">f.</a> natu. bot. <i>Gmelina arborea</i>, arbre gamhar, de la famille des verbénacées, aux feuilles opposées en forme de cœur, aux fleurs en épis brun-jaunes; il peut atteindre 20 m; c'est l'une des 5 racines médicinales majeures [<a href="#entry-b_rhatpa_ncamuula" class="skt-link">bṛhatpañcamūla</a>] <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> sa fleur; son fruit; sa racine.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-gambhan">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">गम्भन्</span> <i>gambhan</i> <a href="#entry-gambhan" class="skt-link">n.</a> profondeur.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-gambhara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">गम्भर</span> <i>gambhara</i> [<a href="#entry-gambhan" class="skt-link">gambhan</a>] <a href="#entry-gambhara" class="skt-link">n.</a> profondeur.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-gambhi_s_tha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">गम्भिष्ठ</span> <i>gambhiṣṭha</i> [super. <a href="#entry-gambhiira" class="skt-link">gambhīra</a>] a. <a href="#entry-gambhi_s_tha" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-gambhi_s_tha" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-gambhi_s_thaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>gambhiṣṭhā</i> le plus profond.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-gambhiira">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">गम्भीर</span> <i>gambhīra</i> var. <i>gabhīra</i> [<a href="#entry-gabha" class="skt-link">gabha</a>] a. <a href="#entry-gambhiira" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-gambhiira" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-gambhiiraa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>gambhīrā</i> profond; grave, impénétrable <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> respectable; solennel, mystérieux, secret <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> géo. np. du temple de Gambhīra à <a href="#entry-Upurii" class="skt-link">Purī</a>, où <a href="#entry-Ucaitanya" class="skt-link">Caitanya</a> passa les dernières années de sa vie.<br> <i>trigambhīra</i> a. m. n. <a href="#entry-trigambhiiraa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>trigambhīrā</i> se dit d'un homme dont le nombril, la voix et le caractère sont profonds.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-gambhiirataa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>gambhīratā</i> [<a href="#entry--taa" class="skt-link">-tā</a>] <a href="#entry-gambhiirataa" class="skt-link">f.</a> profondeur.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-gambhiiratva">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>gambhīratva</i> [<a href="#entry--tva" class="skt-link">-tva</a>] <a href="#entry-gambhiiratva" class="skt-link">n.</a> profondeur.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-gambhiirezvara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>gambhīreśvara</i> [<a href="#entry-iizvara" class="skt-link">īśvara</a>] <a href="#entry-gambhiirezvara" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. de Gambhīreśvara «Seigneur des profondeurs», épith. de <a href="#entry-Uziva" class="skt-link">Śiva</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-gambhiira" class="skt-link">gambhīra</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-gamya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">गम्य</span> <i>gamya</i> [pfp. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] <a href="#entry-gam" class="skt-link">gam</a>] a. <a href="#entry-gamya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-gamya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-gamyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>gamyā</i> que l'on peut fréquenter; dissolu <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> perceptible; accessible; compréhensible.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-gandha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">गन्ध</span> <i>gandha</i> <a href="#entry-gandha" class="skt-link">m.</a> odeur, senteur; parfum <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> phil. [<a href="#entry-saafkhya" class="skt-link">sāṃkhya</a>] l'odeur, perception [<a href="#entry-tanmaatra" class="skt-link">tanmātra</a>] du sens de l'odorat [<a href="#entry-ghraa_na" class="skt-link">ghrāṇa</a>]; il est régi [<a href="#entry-niyant_r" class="skt-link">niyantṛ</a>] par la paire des <a href="#entry-Uazvinau" class="skt-link">Aśvinau</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> phil. [<a href="#entry-vaize_sika" class="skt-link">vaiśeṣika</a>] la qualité [<a href="#entry-gu_na" class="skt-link">guṇa</a>] de l'odeur; on caractérise 9 sortes d'odeurs: <a href="#entry-i_s_ta_1" class="skt-link">iṣṭa_1</a> (agréable), <a href="#entry-ani_s_ta" class="skt-link">aniṣṭa</a> (désagréable), <a href="#entry-madhura" class="skt-link">madhura</a> (sucrée), <a href="#entry-ka_tu" class="skt-link">kaṭu</a> (âcre), <a href="#entry-nirhaarin" class="skt-link">nirhārin</a> (diffusante), <a href="#entry-sa_mhata" class="skt-link">saṃhata</a> (dense), <a href="#entry-snigdha" class="skt-link">snigdha</a> (grasse), <a href="#entry-ruuk_sa" class="skt-link">rūkṣa</a> (rude), <a href="#entry-vizada" class="skt-link">viśada</a> (pure); on ajoute parfois <a href="#entry-amla" class="skt-link">amla</a> (acide) — <a href="#entry-gandhaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>gandhā</i> ifc. qui a l'odeur de <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> juste un parfum de; petite quantité de; petit <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> relation, connection <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> fierté; arrogance — <a href="#entry-gandhaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>gandhā</i> cf. <a href="#entry-gandhapalaazii" class="skt-link">gandhapalāśī</a> — <a href="#entry-gandha" class="skt-link">n.</a> <i>gandha</i> odeur.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-gandhaara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">गन्धार</span> <i>gandhāra</i> <a href="#entry-gandhaara" class="skt-link">m.</a> géo. pays du Gandhāra, au nord-ouest de l'Inde ancienne, de la passe de Kyber à l'Indus; sa capitale était <a href="#entry-Upu_skaraavatii" class="skt-link">Puṣkarāvatī</a>, puis <a href="#entry-Upuru_sapura" class="skt-link">Puruṣapura</a>; son université de <a href="#entry-Utak_sazilaa" class="skt-link">Takṣaśilā</a> était renommée; myth. le roi <a href="#entry-Usubala" class="skt-link">Subala</a> y régna <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> minium; vermillon; syn. <a href="#entry-sinduura" class="skt-link">sindūra</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> pl. <i>gandhārās</i> ses habitants.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-gandhaku_tii">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>gandhakuṭī</i> [<a href="#entry-ku_tii" class="skt-link">kuṭī</a>] <a href="#entry-gandhaku_tii" class="skt-link">f.</a> bd. chambre du <a href="#entry-buddha" class="skt-link">buddha</a> principal d'un monastère.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-gandhamaadana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>gandhamādana</i> [<a href="#entry-maadana" class="skt-link">mādana</a>] a. <a href="#entry-gandhamaadana" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-gandhamaadana" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-gandhamaadanaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>gandhamādanā</i> dont le parfum enivre — <a href="#entry-gandhamaadana" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. du Mont Gandhamādana «aux Parfums enivrants»; à l'est du Mont <a href="#entry-Umeru" class="skt-link">Meru</a>, couvert d'une forêt fragrante; il porte le palais de <a href="#entry-Ukubera" class="skt-link">Kubera</a>; cf. <a href="#entry-Uja_taasura" class="skt-link">Jaṭāsura</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-gandhapalaazii">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>gandhapalāśī</i> [<a href="#entry-palaazii" class="skt-link">palāśī</a>] <a href="#entry-gandhapalaazii" class="skt-link">f.</a> natu. bot. <i>Curcuma zedoaria</i>, variété de gingembre.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-gandharva">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">गन्धर्व</span> <i>gandharva</i> [<a href="#entry-gandha" class="skt-link">gandha</a><a href="#entry--va" class="skt-link">-va</a>] <a href="#entry-gandharva" class="skt-link">m.</a> véd. np. de Gandharva «Parfumé», gardien du <a href="#entry-soma" class="skt-link">soma</a>; il se tient sur le firmament et révèle la Vérité Divine sous la forme d'une pluie céleste; il bêcha l'herbe qui guérit <a href="#entry-Uvaru_na" class="skt-link">Varuṇa</a> de son impuissance et révéla le <a href="#entry-Uveda" class="skt-link">Veda</a> à <a href="#entry-Uvaak" class="skt-link">Vāk</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. (plus tard, collectivement) un génie-<a href="#entry-gandharva" class="skt-link">gandharva</a>, musicien céleste du paradis d'<a href="#entry-Uindra" class="skt-link">Indra</a>; les <i>gandharvās</i> étaient les gardiens du <a href="#entry-soma" class="skt-link">soma</a>, dans le temps mythique précédant la Création; <a href="#entry-Uvaayu" class="skt-link">Vāyu</a> (ou <a href="#entry-Uvaru_na" class="skt-link">Varuṇa</a>) est leur roi; ils sont fils de <a href="#entry-Ukazyapa" class="skt-link">Kaśyapa</a> et de <a href="#entry-Umunii" class="skt-link">Munī</a> (ou issus du nez de <a href="#entry-Ubrahmaa" class="skt-link">Brahmā</a>), et amants infatigables des nymphes [<a href="#entry-apsaras" class="skt-link">apsaras</a>]; ils sont experts en médecine, et peuvent posséder les humains (not. par épilepsie); ils sont associés au fœtus, et sont invoqués dans les rites du mariage; leur mode de mariage [<a href="#entry-gaandharva" class="skt-link">gāndharva</a>] est l'amour clandestin; on les représente avec un torse humain et un corps d'oiseau ou de cheval; ils habitent le ciel, règlent le cours des astres, et sont associés aux <a href="#entry-nak_satraa_ni" class="skt-link">nakṣatrāṇi</a>; cf. not. <a href="#entry-Uafgaarapar_na" class="skt-link">Aṅgāraparṇa</a>, <a href="#entry-Uk_rzaanu" class="skt-link">Kṛśānu</a>, <a href="#entry-Uca_n_davega" class="skt-link">Caṇḍavega</a>, <a href="#entry-Ucitraratha" class="skt-link">Citraratha</a>, <a href="#entry-Ucitrasena" class="skt-link">Citrasena</a>, <a href="#entry-Uti_sya" class="skt-link">Tiṣya</a>, <a href="#entry-Utumburu" class="skt-link">Tumburu</a>, <a href="#entry-Upuruuravaa" class="skt-link">Purūravā</a>, <a href="#entry-Upriya_mvada" class="skt-link">Priyaṃvada</a>, <a href="#entry-Uvizvaavasu" class="skt-link">Viśvāvasu</a>, <a href="#entry-Uzik_sa" class="skt-link">Śikṣa</a>, <a href="#entry-Usaata" class="skt-link">Sāta</a>, <a href="#entry-Uhaahaa" class="skt-link">Hāhā</a>, <a href="#entry-Uhuhu" class="skt-link">Huhu</a>; cf. <a href="#entry-kinnara" class="skt-link">kiṃnara</a> — <a href="#entry-gandharvii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>gandharvī</i> myth. <a href="#entry-gandharva" class="skt-link">gandharva</a> femelle; cf. <a href="#entry-Udundubhii" class="skt-link">Dundubhī</a>, <a href="#entry-Upura_njanii" class="skt-link">Purañjanī</a>, <a href="#entry-Usomadaa" class="skt-link">Somadā</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. np. de Gandharvī, fille de <a href="#entry-Usurabhii" class="skt-link">Surabhī</a>; elle engendra la race des chevaux <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> gr. Κενταυρος; fr. centaure.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-gandharvadatta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>gandharvadatta</i> [<a href="#entry-datta" class="skt-link">datta</a>] a. <a href="#entry-gandharvadatta" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-gandharvadatta" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-gandharvadattaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>gandharvadattā</i> donné par les <a href="#entry-gandharvaas" class="skt-link">gandharvās</a> — <a href="#entry-gandharvadattaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>gandharvadattā</i> lit. [<a href="#entry-Ukathaasaritsaagara" class="skt-link">Kathāsaritsāgara</a>] np. de la belle Gandharvadattā, amante du prince <a href="#entry-Unaravaahanadatta" class="skt-link">Naravāhanadatta</a>, qui la gagna à un concours de <a href="#entry-vii_naa" class="skt-link">vīṇā</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-gandharvag_rhiita">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>gandharvagṛhīta</i> [<a href="#entry-g_rhiita" class="skt-link">gṛhīta</a>] a. <a href="#entry-gandharvag_rhiita" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-gandharvag_rhiita" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-gandharvag_rhiitaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>gandharvagṛhītā</i> possédé par un <a href="#entry-gandharva" class="skt-link">gandharva</a>; en transe <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> épileptique.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-gandharvagraha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>gandharvagraha</i> [<a href="#entry-graha" class="skt-link">graha</a>] <a href="#entry-gandharvagraha" class="skt-link">m.</a> phil. [<a href="#entry-tantra" class="skt-link">tantra</a>] possession par un centaure.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-gandharvanagara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>gandharvanagara</i> [<a href="#entry-nagara" class="skt-link">nagara</a>] <a href="#entry-gandharvanagara" class="skt-link">n.</a> myth. splendide ville céleste des <a href="#entry-gandharvaas" class="skt-link">gandharvās</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> mirage.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-gandharvayak_sa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>gandharvayakṣa</i> [<a href="#entry-yak_sa" class="skt-link">yakṣa</a>] <a href="#entry-gandharvayak_sa" class="skt-link">m.</a> pl. <i>gandharvayakṣās</i> myth. les génies du Ciel [<a href="#entry-gandharva" class="skt-link">gandharva</a>] et de la Terre [<a href="#entry-yak_sa" class="skt-link">yakṣa</a>].<br> <a href="#entry-gandharva" class="skt-link">gandharva</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-gandhavaha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>gandhavaha</i> [<a href="#entry-vaha" class="skt-link">vaha</a>] <a href="#entry-gandhavaha" class="skt-link">m.</a> vent [«qui porte les odeurs»].
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-gandhavat">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>gandhavat</i> [<a href="#entry--vat" class="skt-link">-vat</a>] a. <a href="#entry-gandhavat" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-gandhavat" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-gandhavatii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>gandhavatī</i> parfumé — <a href="#entry-gandhavatii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>gandhavatī</i> myth. [<a href="#entry-Umahaabhaarata" class="skt-link">Mah.</a>] np. de Gandhavatī «la Parfumée», épith. de <a href="#entry-Usatyavatii" class="skt-link">Satyavatī</a> après que <a href="#entry-Uparaazara" class="skt-link">Parāśara</a> l'eut débarrassée de son odeur de poisson; cf. <a href="#entry-Ukastuuriigandhii" class="skt-link">Kastūrīgandhī</a>.<br> <i>gandhavattvāṃ</i> <i>pṛthivyā</i> <i>lakṣaṇam</i> phil. [<a href="#entry-nyaaya" class="skt-link">nyāya</a>] La qualité d'avoir une odeur est le signe caractéristique de la terre.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-gandhayukti">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>gandhayukti</i> [<a href="#entry-yukti" class="skt-link">yukti</a>] <a href="#entry-gandhayukti" class="skt-link">f.</a> la composition des parfums, l'un des 64 arts [<a href="#entry-kalaa" class="skt-link">kalā</a>].
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-gandhazaastra">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>gandhaśāstra</i> [<a href="#entry-zaastra" class="skt-link">śāstra</a>] <a href="#entry-gandhazaastra" class="skt-link">n.</a> lit. science des cosmétiques et des parfums.<br> <a href="#entry-gandha" class="skt-link">gandha</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-gandhi">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">गन्धि</span> <i>gandhi</i> [<a href="#entry-gandhin" class="skt-link">gandhin</a>] ifc. a. <a href="#entry-gandhi" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-gandhi" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-gandhi" class="skt-link">f.</a> parfumé à; qui sent le <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> évocateur de; qui ne contient que des traces de; ⟨iic.⟩ seulement de nom.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-gandhin">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">गन्धिन्</span> <i>gandhin</i> [<a href="#entry-gandha" class="skt-link">gandha</a><a href="#entry--in" class="skt-link">-in</a>] a. <a href="#entry-gandhin" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-gandhin" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-gandhinii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>gandhinī</i> parfumé; odorant — ifc. qui sent le, parfumé à <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> évocateur de; qui ne contient que des traces de; ⟨iic.⟩ seulement de nom.<br> <i>mātṛgandhinī</i> une mère seulement de nom.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-gantavya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">गन्तव्य</span> <i>gantavya</i> [pfp. [<span class="skt-grammar">3</span>] <a href="#entry-gam" class="skt-link">gam</a>] a. <a href="#entry-gantavya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-gantavya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-gantavyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>gantavyā</i> ce vers quoi on va; où l'on doit aller; à atteindre; qui doit être accompli ou entrepris ou subi; avec qui l'on peut avoir des rapports charnels; qu'on peut accuser de ⟨i.⟩ <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> qui va se produire, imminent; inéluctable <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> (impers.) il faut y aller.<br> <i>mayā</i> <i>gantavyam</i> il faut que j'y aille.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-gara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">गर</span> <i>gara</i> [<a href="#entry-g_rr_1" class="skt-link">gṝ_1</a>] <a href="#entry-gara" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-gara" class="skt-link">n.</a> [«ce qu'on avale»] boisson empoisonnée <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> astr. cinquième demi-jour lunaire [<a href="#entry-kara_na" class="skt-link">karaṇa</a>] (mobile).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-garbha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">गर्भ</span> <i>garbha</i> [<a href="#entry-grah" class="skt-link">grah</a>] <a href="#entry-garbha" class="skt-link">m.</a> intérieur de qqc.; sanctuaire <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> matrice; sein maternel; ventre <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> germe vivant; embryon, fœtus <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> soc. dépôt consacré dans un temple en construction <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> [<i>garbhasandhi</i>] lit. [théâtre] nœud de l'intrigue; c'est l'une des 5 articulations de récits dramatiques [<a href="#entry-pa_ncasandhi" class="skt-link">pañcasandhi</a>] — <a href="#entry-garbhaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>garbhā</i> ifc. contenant, renfermant; plein de, lourd de <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> all. kalb; ang. calf.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-garbhaadhaana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>garbhādhāna</i> [<a href="#entry-aadhaana" class="skt-link">ādhāna</a>] <a href="#entry-garbhaadhaana" class="skt-link">n.</a> imprégnation (de ⟨loc.⟩) <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> soc. accueil de l'embryon, un rite [<a href="#entry-sa_mskaara" class="skt-link">saṃskāra</a>] de fertilité entre époux effectué avant la conception.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-garbhaazaya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>garbhāśaya</i> [<a href="#entry-aazaya" class="skt-link">āśaya</a>] <a href="#entry-garbhaazaya" class="skt-link">m.</a> méd. utérus.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-garbhag_rha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>garbhagṛha</i> [<a href="#entry-g_rha" class="skt-link">gṛha</a>] <a href="#entry-garbhag_rha" class="skt-link">n.</a> appartement intérieur; sanctuaire d'un temple, <i>cella</i>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-garbhanirodhaka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>garbhanirodhaka</i> [<a href="#entry-nirodhaka" class="skt-link">nirodhaka</a>] <a href="#entry-garbhanirodhaka" class="skt-link">m.</a> mod. préservatif.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-garbhasraava">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>garbhasrāva</i> [<a href="#entry-sraava" class="skt-link">srāva</a>] <a href="#entry-garbhasraava" class="skt-link">m.</a> fausse couche; avortement.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-garbhastha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>garbhastha</i> [<a href="#entry-stha" class="skt-link">stha</a>] a. <a href="#entry-garbhastha" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-garbhastha" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-garbhasthaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>garbhasthā</i> à l'intérieur de la matrice (embryon) <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> à l'intérieur de ⟨g.⟩.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-garbhasthasamaasa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>garbhasthasamāsa</i> [<a href="#entry-samaasa" class="skt-link">samāsa</a>] <a href="#entry-garbhasthasamaasa" class="skt-link">m.</a> gram. mot composé interne à un autre mot composé (celui-ci étant dit <a href="#entry-samaasagarbhita" class="skt-link">samāsagarbhita</a>).<br> <a href="#entry-garbhastha" class="skt-link">garbhastha</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-garbhin">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">गर्भिन्</span> <i>garbhin</i> [<a href="#entry-garbha" class="skt-link">garbha</a><a href="#entry--in" class="skt-link">-in</a>] a. <a href="#entry-garbhin" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-garbhin" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-garbhi_nii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>garbhiṇī</i> rempli de ⟨acc. i.⟩; fécondé — <a href="#entry-garbhi_nii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>garbhiṇī</i> femme enceinte — <a href="#entry-garbhi_nii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>garbhiṇī</i> ifc. animal ⟨iic.⟩ femelle fécondée.<br> <i>gogarbhiṇī</i> vache pleine.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-garbhita">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">गर्भित</span> <i>garbhita</i> [<a href="#entry-garbha" class="skt-link">garbha</a><a href="#entry--ita" class="skt-link">-ita</a>] a. <a href="#entry-garbhita" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-garbhita" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-garbhitaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>garbhitā</i> interne; emboîté; incrusté, inséré, encapsulé — ifc. rempli par; contenant.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-garbhitataa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>garbhitatā</i> [<a href="#entry--taa" class="skt-link">-tā</a>] <a href="#entry-garbhitataa" class="skt-link">f.</a> rhét. emboîtement d'une phrase à l'intérieur d'une autre.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-garbhitatva">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>garbhitatva</i> [<a href="#entry--tva" class="skt-link">-tva</a>] <a href="#entry-garbhitatva" class="skt-link">n.</a> id.<br> <a href="#entry-garbhita" class="skt-link">garbhita</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-garbhodaka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>garbhodaka</i> [<a href="#entry-udaka" class="skt-link">udaka</a>] <a href="#entry-garbhodaka" class="skt-link">n.</a> méd. eaux amniotiques <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. np. de Garbhodaka, grand océan primordial de l'Univers, où demeure <a href="#entry-Uze_sa" class="skt-link">Śeṣa</a> après l'anéantissement des mondes [<a href="#entry-pralaya" class="skt-link">pralaya</a>]; [BP.] <a href="#entry-Uhira_nyaak_sa" class="skt-link">Hiraṇyākṣa</a> y plongea la Terre, qui fut sauvée des Eaux par <a href="#entry-Uvi_s_nu" class="skt-link">Viṣṇu</a>-<a href="#entry-Uvaraaha" class="skt-link">Varāha</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-garbhodakazaayin">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>garbhodakaśāyin</i> [<a href="#entry-zaayin" class="skt-link">śāyin</a>] <a href="#entry-garbhodakazaayin" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. de Garbhodakaśāyī, épith. de <a href="#entry-Uvi_s_nu" class="skt-link">Viṣṇu</a> «Reposant sur les Eaux primordiales».<br> <a href="#entry-garbhodaka" class="skt-link">garbhodaka</a> <br> <a href="#entry-garbha" class="skt-link">garbha</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-gard">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">गर्द्</span> <i>gard</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. <i>(gardati)</i> crier; braire.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-garda">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">गर्द</span> <i>garda</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-gard" class="skt-link">gard</a>] a. <a href="#entry-garda" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-garda" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-gardaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>gardā</i> qui crie; qui brait.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-gardabha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>gardabha</i> [<a href="#entry-bha" class="skt-link">bha</a>] <a href="#entry-gardabha" class="skt-link">m.</a> natu. zoo. âne; syn. <a href="#entry-khara" class="skt-link">khara</a>, <a href="#entry-raasabha" class="skt-link">rāsabha</a> — <a href="#entry-gardabhii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>gardabhī</i> natu. ânesse.<br> <a href="#entry-garda" class="skt-link">garda</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-gardha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">गर्ध</span> <i>gardha</i> [act. <a href="#entry-g_rdh" class="skt-link">gṛdh</a>] ifc. <a href="#entry-gardha" class="skt-link">m.</a> désir de, envie de.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-gardhin">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">गर्धिन्</span> <i>gardhin</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-g_rdh" class="skt-link">gṛdh</a>] a. <a href="#entry-gardhin" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-gardhin" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-gardhinii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>gardhinī</i> avide de; qui désire; qui se languit de.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-garga">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">गर्ग</span> <i>garga</i> <a href="#entry-garga" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. du sage [<a href="#entry-_r_si" class="skt-link">ṛṣi</a>] Garga <a href="#entry-Ubhaaradvaaja" class="skt-link">Bhāradvāja</a>; [BhP.] il était chapelain [<a href="#entry-purohita" class="skt-link">purohita</a>] des princes <a href="#entry-yaadavaas" class="skt-link">yādavās</a>, et consacra <a href="#entry-Uk_r_s_na" class="skt-link">Kṛṣṇa</a> et <a href="#entry-Ubalaraama" class="skt-link">Balarāma</a> enfants à <a href="#entry-Uvraja" class="skt-link">Vraja</a> — <a href="#entry-gargii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>gargī</i> [<i>gārgī</i>] lit. [BU.] np. de Gargī, philosophe antique, fille du sage <a href="#entry-Uvacaknu" class="skt-link">Vacaknu</a>; elle posa les questions les plus difficiles sur l'Absolu [<a href="#entry-brahman" class="skt-link">brahman</a>] à <a href="#entry-Uyaaj_navalkya" class="skt-link">Yājñavalkya</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-garh">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">गर्ह्</span> <i>garh</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. <i>(garhati)</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">10</span>] pr. <i>(garhayati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>gharita</i><i>)</i> se plaindre de (à ⟨dat.⟩); accuser, blâmer.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-garha_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">गर्हण</span> <i>garhaṇa</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-garh" class="skt-link">garh</a>] a. <a href="#entry-garha_na" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-garha_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-garha_naa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>garhaṇā</i> impliquant un reproche — <a href="#entry-garha_na" class="skt-link">m.</a> blâme, reproche; censure — <a href="#entry-garha_naa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>garhaṇā</i> id.<br> <i>garhaṇāṃ</i> <i>yā</i> encourir le reproche.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-garha_niiya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">गर्हणीय</span> <i>garhaṇīya</i> [pfp. [<span class="skt-grammar">2</span>] <a href="#entry-garh" class="skt-link">garh</a>] a. <a href="#entry-garha_niiya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-garha_niiya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-garha_niiyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>garhaṇīyā</i> blâmable; à blâmer.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-garhita">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">गर्हित</span> <i>garhita</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-garh" class="skt-link">garh</a>] a. <a href="#entry-garhita" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-garhita" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-garhitaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>garhitā</i> blâmé (par ⟨i. g. loc. iic.⟩).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-gari_s_tha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">गरिष्ठ</span> <i>gariṣṭha</i> [super. <a href="#entry-guru" class="skt-link">guru</a>] a. <a href="#entry-gari_s_tha" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-gari_s_tha" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-gari_s_thaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>gariṣṭhā</i> très lourd; le plus lourd <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> très important; le plus important.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-gariiyas">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">गरीयस्</span> <i>garīyas</i> [compar. <a href="#entry-guru" class="skt-link">guru</a>] a. <a href="#entry-gariiyas" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-gariiyas" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-gariiyasii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>garīyasī</i> plus lourd que, plus grand que, plus important que ⟨abl.⟩; plus cher que <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> très lourd, très grand; très vénérable — <a href="#entry-gariiyas" class="skt-link">n.</a> grand poids; importance.<br> <i>apisvarṇamayī</i> <i>laṅkā</i> <i>na</i> <i>me</i> <i>lakṣmaṇa</i> <i>rocate</i> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> <i>jananī</i> <i>janmabhūmiśca</i> <i>svargādapi</i> <i>garīyasī</i> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> Quand bien même Ceylan serait toute en or, Ô <a href="#entry-Ulak_sma_na" class="skt-link">Lakṣmaṇa</a>, elle ne pourrait me plaire, car la mère et la patrie sont préférables même au paradis.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-gariman">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">गरिमन्</span> <i>gariman</i> [<a href="#entry-guru" class="skt-link">guru</a><a href="#entry--man" class="skt-link">-man</a>] <a href="#entry-gariman" class="skt-link">m.</a> poids, lourdeur; importance, respectabilité — <a href="#entry-garimaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>garimā</i> phil. [<a href="#entry-yoga" class="skt-link">yoga</a>] le pouvoir de devenir lourd, un <a href="#entry-siddhi" class="skt-link">siddhi</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-garj">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">गर्ज्</span> <i>garj</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. <i>(garjati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>garjita</i><i>)</i> gronder (tonnerre); grogner, rugir, barrir.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-garja">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">गर्ज</span> <i>garja</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-garj" class="skt-link">garj</a>] <a href="#entry-garja" class="skt-link">m.</a> éléphant qui barrit <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> barrissement; grondement (orage).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-garjana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">गर्जन</span> <i>garjana</i> [act. <a href="#entry-garj" class="skt-link">garj</a>] <a href="#entry-garjana" class="skt-link">n.</a> grondement d'orage, roulement de tonnerre <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> grognement; barrissement, rugissement.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-garjita">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">गर्जित</span> <i>garjita</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-garj" class="skt-link">garj</a>] a. <a href="#entry-garjita" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-garjita" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-garjitaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>garjitā</i> qui a grondé ou grogné — <a href="#entry-garjita" class="skt-link">n.</a> tonnerre; cri, grognement; barrissement.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-garta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">गर्त</span> <i>garta</i> <a href="#entry-garta" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-garta" class="skt-link">n.</a> trou, fosse; caverne.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-garu_da">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">गरुड</span> <i>garuḍa</i> [<a href="#entry-g_rr_1" class="skt-link">gṝ_1</a>] <a href="#entry-garu_da" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. de l'aigle mythique Garuḍa «(Soleil) qui Dévore», fils de <a href="#entry-Ukazyapa" class="skt-link">Kaśyapa</a> et <a href="#entry-Uvinataa" class="skt-link">Vinatā</a>, frère cadet d'<a href="#entry-Uaru_na" class="skt-link">Aruṇa</a> [<a href="#entry-aru_naanuja" class="skt-link">aruṇānuja</a>]; à sa naissance il effraya les dieux par son éclat; il est roi des oiseaux et destructeur des serpents dont il est affamé (<a href="#entry-Unaagaantaka" class="skt-link">Nāgāntaka</a>, <a href="#entry-Unaagaazana" class="skt-link">Nāgāśana</a>); [<a href="#entry-Umahaabhaarata" class="skt-link">Mah.</a>] <a href="#entry-Ukazyapa" class="skt-link">Kaśyapa</a> lui montra au fond d'un lac un éléphant et une tortue occupés à se battre pour un héritage, et qu'il dévora; il déroba l'ambroisie [<a href="#entry-am_rta" class="skt-link">amṛta</a>] pour le donner aux serpents qui tenaient sa mère prisonnière, mais une ruse permit à <a href="#entry-Uindra" class="skt-link">Indra</a> de le reprendre; il est la monture [<a href="#entry-vaahana" class="skt-link">vāhana</a>] de <a href="#entry-Uvi_s_nu" class="skt-link">Viṣṇu</a> dont il obtint l'immortalité pourvu qu'il ne boive pas l'<a href="#entry-am_rta" class="skt-link">amṛta</a>; il est père de <a href="#entry-Uvaalmiiki" class="skt-link">Vālmīki</a> et de <a href="#entry-Udiipaka" class="skt-link">Dīpaka</a>; il protège des poisons; cf. <a href="#entry-Utaark_sya" class="skt-link">Tārkṣya</a>, <a href="#entry-Uvainateya" class="skt-link">Vainateya</a>, <a href="#entry-Usupar_na" class="skt-link">Suparṇa</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-garu_dadhvaja">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>garuḍadhvaja</i> [<a href="#entry-dhvaja" class="skt-link">dhvaja</a>] <a href="#entry-garu_dadhvaja" class="skt-link">m.</a> arch. colonne de support de <a href="#entry-Ugaru_da" class="skt-link">Garuḍa</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-garu_dapuraa_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>garuḍapurāṇa</i> [<a href="#entry-puraa_na" class="skt-link">purāṇa</a>] <a href="#entry-garu_dapuraa_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> soc. np. du Garuḍapurāṇa ou Gāruḍapurāṇa, ouvrage traditionnel encyclopédique d'obédience <a href="#entry-vai_s_nava" class="skt-link">vaiṣṇava</a>, en 200 chapitres [<a href="#entry-adhyaaya" class="skt-link">adhyāya</a>]; il traite des rites et festivals, des cérémonies expiatoires, de la glorification des lieux saints, mais aussi de science, médecine, divination, grammaire et politique; cf. <a href="#entry-saattvika" class="skt-link">sāttvika</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-garu_da" class="skt-link">garuḍa</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-garut">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">गरुत्</span> <i>garut</i> <a href="#entry-garut" class="skt-link">m.</a> aile.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-garutmat">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>garutmat</i> [<a href="#entry--mat" class="skt-link">-mat</a>] <a href="#entry-garutmat" class="skt-link">m.</a> aigle <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. np. de Garutmān, le grand aigle <a href="#entry-Ugaru_da" class="skt-link">Garuḍa</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-garut" class="skt-link">garut</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-garva">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">गर्व</span> <i>garva</i> <a href="#entry-garva" class="skt-link">m.</a> orgueil, vanité; arrogance <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> [théâtre] discours arrogant — v. [<span class="skt-grammar">11</span>] pr. <i>(garvayati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>garvita</i><i>)</i> rendre orgueilleux — v. [<span class="skt-grammar">11</span>] pr. md. <i>(garvāyate)</i> s'enorgueillir, être vaniteux; être arrogant.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-garvin">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">गर्विन्</span> <i>garvin</i> [<a href="#entry-garva" class="skt-link">garva</a><a href="#entry--in" class="skt-link">-in</a>] a. <a href="#entry-garvin" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-garvin" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-garvi_nii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>garviṇī</i> homme vaniteux; fat.<br> <i>alpavidyo</i> <i>mahāgarvī</i> [SRB.] Les sots sont excessivement vaniteux.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-garvita">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">गर्वित</span> <i>garvita</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-garva" class="skt-link">garva</a>] a. <a href="#entry-garvita" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-garvita" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-garvitaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>garvitā</i> orgueilleux, vaniteux, hautain; fier de ⟨i. iic.⟩ — <a href="#entry-garvita" class="skt-link">n.</a> orgueil.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-gata">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">गत</span> <i>gata</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-gam" class="skt-link">gam</a>] a. <a href="#entry-gata" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-gata" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-gataa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>gatā</i> allé, parti; passé, révolu, perdu; venu, arrivé <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> qui va jusqu'à, se rendant à ⟨acc.⟩; sorti, issu de ⟨abl. iic.⟩ — <a href="#entry-gata" class="skt-link">n.</a> mise en route, départ; arrivée <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> marche, mouvement <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> le passé.<br> <i>ātmagatam</i> var. <i>svagatam</i> adv. à part soi, à part.<br> <i>gatagatā</i> elle est partie, hélas!
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-gataasu">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>gatāsu</i> [<a href="#entry-asu" class="skt-link">asu</a>] a. <a href="#entry-gataasu" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-gataasu" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-gataasu" class="skt-link">f.</a> [«qui a expiré»] mort.<br> <i>gatāsūnagatāsūṃśca</i> <i>nānuśocanti</i> <i>paṇḍitāḥ</i> [BhG.] Les sages ne pleurent ni les morts ni les vivants.<br> <a href="#entry-gata" class="skt-link">gata</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-gataayus">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>gatāyus</i> [<a href="#entry-aayus" class="skt-link">āyus</a>] a. <a href="#entry-gataayus" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-gataayus" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-gataayus" class="skt-link">f.</a> mort [«dont l'énergie vitale est partie»].
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-gatavat">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">गतवत्</span> <i>gatavat</i> [ppa. <a href="#entry-gam" class="skt-link">gam</a>] a. <a href="#entry-gatavat" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-gatavat" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-gatavatii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>gatavatī</i> qui passe; qui ressent.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-gatazriika">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>gataśrīka</i> [<a href="#entry-zrii" class="skt-link">śrī</a><a href="#entry--ka" class="skt-link">-ka</a>] a. <a href="#entry-gatazriika" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-gatazriika" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-gatazriikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>gataśrīkā</i> qui a perdu sa prospérité, ruiné.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-gati">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">गति</span> <i>gati</i> [act. <a href="#entry-gam" class="skt-link">gam</a>] <a href="#entry-gati" class="skt-link">f.</a> marche, démarche, allure; cours, destination; pouvoir <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> accès, issue, refuge; moyen; expédient <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> origine, source; état, condition; situation (dans une vie future), destinée <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> gram. préposition utilisée comme préverbe co-référentiel à l'action du verbe; sont aussi <a href="#entry-gati" class="skt-link">gati</a> quelques particules comme <a href="#entry-alam" class="skt-link">alam</a> et les formes inchoatives en <i>-ī</i> (<i>cvi</i>) <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> ang. gait.<br> <i>grahāṇām</i> <i>gati</i> trajet des planètes.<br> <i>anyā</i> <i>gatirnāsti</i> il n'y a pas d'autre alternative.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-gaticitra">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>gaticitra</i> [<a href="#entry-citra" class="skt-link">citra</a>] <a href="#entry-gaticitra" class="skt-link">n.</a> lit. variété de <a href="#entry-citrakaavya" class="skt-link">citrakāvya</a>, où le poème est lu par une suite de mouvements dans des rangées de lettres; cf. <a href="#entry-gomuutrikaa" class="skt-link">gomūtrikā</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-gatihiina">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>gatihīna</i> [<a href="#entry-hiina" class="skt-link">hīna</a>] a. <a href="#entry-gatihiina" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-gatihiina" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-gatihiinaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>gatihīnā</i> abandonné, sans refuge.<br> <a href="#entry-gati" class="skt-link">gati</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-gatimat">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>gatimat</i> [<a href="#entry--mat" class="skt-link">-mat</a>] a. <a href="#entry-gatimat" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-gatimat" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-gatimatii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>gatimatī</i> qui se déplace, mobile <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> gram. muni d'un préverbe.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-gatipracaara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>gatipracāra</i> [<a href="#entry-pracaara" class="skt-link">pracāra</a>] <a href="#entry-gatipracaara" class="skt-link">m.</a> pratique des postures (de dance, de <a href="#entry-yoga" class="skt-link">yoga</a>, de rites, etc.) <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> lit. section du <a href="#entry-Unaa_tyazaastra" class="skt-link">Nāṭyaśāstra</a> sur les postures.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-gatitva">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>gatitva</i> [<a href="#entry--tva" class="skt-link">-tva</a>] <a href="#entry-gatitva" class="skt-link">n.</a> mobilité.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-gau_da">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">गौड</span> <i>gauḍa</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-gu_da" class="skt-link">guḍa</a>] a. <a href="#entry-gau_da" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-gau_da" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-gau_dii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>gauḍī</i> fait de sucre, de mélasse — <a href="#entry-gau_da" class="skt-link">m.</a> [<i>gauḍadeśa</i>] géo. nom du pays Gauḍa (Bengale central) <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> lit. style poétique [<a href="#entry-riiti" class="skt-link">rīti</a>] du <a href="#entry-Ugau_da" class="skt-link">Gauḍa</a>; opp. <a href="#entry-vaidarbha" class="skt-link">vaidarbha</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> pl. <i>gauḍās</i> les Gauḍās, habitants du Bengale de l'Ouest <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> soc. (au sens large) indiens du Nord; cf. <a href="#entry-pa_ncagau_da" class="skt-link">pañcagauḍa</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> soc. doctrine de la mouvance [<a href="#entry-sampradaaya" class="skt-link">saṃpradāya</a>] du Bengale [<a href="#entry-gau_diiya" class="skt-link">gauḍīya</a>].
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-gau_dapaada">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>gauḍapāda</i> [<a href="#entry-paada" class="skt-link">pāda</a>] <a href="#entry-gau_dapaada" class="skt-link">m.</a> hist. np. du philosophe bengali Gauḍapāda (8⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ siècle), commentateur de plusieurs leçons [<a href="#entry-upani_sad" class="skt-link">upaniṣad</a>], proche du bouddhisme; il fut le précepteur de <a href="#entry-Ugovindanaatha" class="skt-link">Govindanātha</a>; c'est un précurseur de l'<a href="#entry-advaita" class="skt-link">advaita</a> de <a href="#entry-Uzafkara_1" class="skt-link">Śaṅkara_1</a>; on lui doit not. l'enseignement des <a href="#entry-Ugau_dapaadakaarikaas" class="skt-link">Gauḍapādakārikās</a> et la <a href="#entry-Umaa_n_duukyakaarikaa" class="skt-link">Māṇḍūkyakārikā</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-gau_dapaadakaarikaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>gauḍapādakārikā</i> [<a href="#entry-kaarikaa" class="skt-link">kārikā</a>] <a href="#entry-gau_dapaadakaarikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> pl. lit. np. des Gauḍapādakārikās, recueil de 215 vers enseignant le <a href="#entry-vedaanta" class="skt-link">vedānta</a> et expliquant l'<a href="#entry-advaita" class="skt-link">advaita</a> par l'universalité de l'<a href="#entry-aatman" class="skt-link">ātman</a>; on les attribue à <a href="#entry-Ugau_dapaada" class="skt-link">Gauḍapāda</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-gau_dapaadiiya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>gauḍapādīya</i> [<a href="#entry--iiya" class="skt-link">-īya</a>] a. <a href="#entry-gau_dapaadiiya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-gau_dapaadiiya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-gau_dapaadiiyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>gauḍapādīyā</i> lit. relatif à <a href="#entry-Ugau_dapaada" class="skt-link">Gauḍapāda</a>; not. <a href="#entry-gau_dapaadiiya" class="skt-link">gauḍapādīya</a> <a href="#entry-kaarikaa" class="skt-link">kārikā</a>, cf. <a href="#entry-Ugau_dapaadakaarikaas" class="skt-link">Gauḍapādakārikās</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-gau_dapaada" class="skt-link">gauḍapāda</a> <br> <a href="#entry-gau_da" class="skt-link">gauḍa</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-gau_diiya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">गौडीय</span> <i>gauḍīya</i> [<a href="#entry-gau_da" class="skt-link">gauḍa</a><a href="#entry--iiya" class="skt-link">-īya</a>] a. <a href="#entry-gau_diiya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-gau_diiya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-gau_diiyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>gauḍīyā</i> relatif au Bengale <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> soc. se dit du mouvement [<a href="#entry-sampradaaya" class="skt-link">saṃpradāya</a>] <a href="#entry-vai_s_nava" class="skt-link">vaiṣṇava</a> bengali prônant la dévotion [<a href="#entry-bhakti" class="skt-link">bhakti</a>], initié par <a href="#entry-Ucaitanya" class="skt-link">Caitanya</a>; l'ouvrage doctrinal principal de cette secte est le <a href="#entry-Ubhaagavatapuraa_na" class="skt-link">Bhāgavatapurāṇa</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-gau_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">गौण</span> <i>gauṇa</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-gu_na" class="skt-link">guṇa</a>] a. <a href="#entry-gau_na" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-gau_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-gau_nii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>gauṇī</i> relatif aux trois qualités [<a href="#entry-gu_na" class="skt-link">guṇa</a>] <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> accessoire, secondaire <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> gram. (substantif) objet secondaire d'une phrase; opp. <a href="#entry-pradhaana" class="skt-link">pradhāna</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> lit. métaphorique — <a href="#entry-gau_nii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>gauṇī</i> lit. sens [<a href="#entry-v_rtti" class="skt-link">vṛtti</a>] métaphorique d'un mot.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-gau_naartha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>gauṇārtha</i> [<a href="#entry-artha" class="skt-link">artha</a>] <a href="#entry-gau_naartha" class="skt-link">n.</a> gram. sens méthaphorique d'un mot; opp. <a href="#entry-mukhyaartha" class="skt-link">mukhyārtha</a> — a. <a href="#entry-gau_naartha" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-gau_naartha" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-gau_naarthaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>gauṇārthā</i> gram. utilisé dans son sens métaphorique.<br> <a href="#entry-gau_na" class="skt-link">gauṇa</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-gau_nakarma">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>gauṇakarma</i> [<a href="#entry-karma" class="skt-link">karma</a>] <a href="#entry-gau_nakarma" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-gau_nakarma" class="skt-link">n.</a> gram. rôle sémantique de patient secondaire (des verbes à deux accusatifs).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-gau_natva">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>gauṇatva</i> [<a href="#entry--tva" class="skt-link">-tva</a>] <a href="#entry-gau_natva" class="skt-link">n.</a> subsidiarité.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-gau_nika">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">गौणिक</span> <i>gauṇika</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-gu_na" class="skt-link">guṇa</a><a href="#entry--ika" class="skt-link">-ika</a>] a. <a href="#entry-gau_nika" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-gau_nika" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-gau_nikii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>gauṇikī</i> relatif aux trois qualités <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> possédant des qualités <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> subsidiaire.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-gaulak_sa_nika">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">गौलक्षणिक</span> <i>gaulakṣaṇika</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-golak_sa_na" class="skt-link">golakṣaṇa</a><a href="#entry--ika" class="skt-link">-ika</a>] a. <a href="#entry-gaulak_sa_nika" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-gaulak_sa_nika" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-gaulak_sa_nikii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>gaulakṣaṇikī</i> soc. qui étudie les marques sur la robe d'une vache.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-gaupavana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">गौपवन</span> <i>gaupavana</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-gopavana" class="skt-link">gopavana</a>] a. <a href="#entry-gaupavana" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-gaupavana" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-gaupavanaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>gaupavanā</i> qui descend de <a href="#entry-Ugopavana" class="skt-link">Gopavana</a> — <a href="#entry-gaupavana" class="skt-link">m.</a> pl. <i>gaupavanāḥ</i> les descendants de <a href="#entry-Ugopavana" class="skt-link">Gopavana</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-gaura">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">गौर</span> <i>gaura</i> a. <a href="#entry-gaura" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-gaura" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-gaurii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>gaurī</i> blanc, pâle; de complexion claire; jaune, blond; fauve; brillant <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> gars, l'ami (adresse familière) <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> natu. zoo. <i>Bos Gaurus</i>, bœuf gaur — <a href="#entry-gaurii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>gaurī</i> fillette (non nubile); not. fillette de 8 ans <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. np. de Gaurī, épith. de <a href="#entry-Upaarvatii" class="skt-link">Pārvatī</a> «au Teint de lait» <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> lit. [<a href="#entry-Ukaadambarii" class="skt-link">Kādambarī</a>] np. de la nymphe [<a href="#entry-apsaras" class="skt-link">apsaras</a>] Gaurī_1, mère de <a href="#entry-Umahaazvetaa" class="skt-link">Mahāśvetā</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> natu. vache gaur.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-gauraafga">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>gaurāṅga</i> [<a href="#entry-afga_1" class="skt-link">aṅga_1</a>] a. <a href="#entry-gauraafga" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-gauraafga" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-gauraafgii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>gaurāṅgī</i> au corps clair; au corps brillant — <a href="#entry-gauraafga" class="skt-link">m.</a> hist. np. de Gaurāṅga, épith. de <a href="#entry-Ucaitanya" class="skt-link">Caitanya</a> «Au corps d'or» — <a href="#entry-gauraafgii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>gaurāṅgī</i> cardamome.<br> <a href="#entry-gaura" class="skt-link">gaura</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-gauracandra">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>gauracandra</i> [<a href="#entry-candra" class="skt-link">candra</a>] <a href="#entry-gauracandra" class="skt-link">m.</a> hist. np. de Gauracandra, épith. de <a href="#entry-Ucaitanya" class="skt-link">Caitanya</a> «Brillant comme la Lune».
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-gaurapuur_nimaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>gaurapūrṇimā</i> [<a href="#entry-puur_nimaa" class="skt-link">pūrṇimā</a>] <a href="#entry-gaurapuur_nimaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> soc. festival célébrant la naissance de <a href="#entry-Ucaitanya" class="skt-link">Caitanya</a>; il se tient début mars, à la pleine lune de <a href="#entry-phaalgunii" class="skt-link">phālgunī</a>; cf. <a href="#entry-vasantotsava" class="skt-link">vasantotsava</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-gaurava">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">गौरव</span> <i>gaurava</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-guru" class="skt-link">guru</a>] a. <a href="#entry-gaurava" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-gaurava" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-gauravaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>gauravā</i> relatif au maître [<a href="#entry-guru" class="skt-link">guru</a>] — <a href="#entry-gaurava" class="skt-link">n.</a> poids, masse; gravité; importance, respectabilité; dignité <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> phon. fait pour une voyelle ou une syllabe d'être longue; opp. <a href="#entry-laaghava" class="skt-link">lāghava</a> — ifc. respect pour.<br> <i>pūrṇatā</i> <i>gauravaya</i> [<a href="#entry-Umeghaduuta" class="skt-link">Meghadūta</a>] Plein respect pour le maître (devise de l'Université sanskrite de <a href="#entry-Usomanaatha" class="skt-link">Somanātha</a>).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-gautama">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">गौतम</span> <i>gautama</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-gotama" class="skt-link">gotama</a>] <a href="#entry-gautama" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. (patr.) descendant de <a href="#entry-Ugotama" class="skt-link">Gotama</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. np. du <a href="#entry-saptar_si" class="skt-link">saptarṣi</a> Gautama, fils de l'<a href="#entry-afgiras" class="skt-link">aṅgiras</a> <a href="#entry-Udiirghatamaa" class="skt-link">Dīrghatamā</a>; il fut le <a href="#entry-guru" class="skt-link">guru</a> d'<a href="#entry-Uindra" class="skt-link">Indra</a>; il épousa <a href="#entry-Uahalyaa" class="skt-link">Ahalyā</a>, qu'il maudit après qu'elle eût commis l'adultère avec <a href="#entry-Uindra" class="skt-link">Indra</a>; il jeta un sort à ce dernier, dont le corps se couvrit de 1000 vagins [<a href="#entry-yoni" class="skt-link">yoni</a>], que les brâhmanes transformèrent en 1000 yeux; il fit aussi tomber ses testicules, qui furent remplacés par les dieux par ceux d'un bélier; <a href="#entry-Uzataananda" class="skt-link">Śatānanda</a> est son fils aîné; cf. <a href="#entry-Utrita" class="skt-link">Trita</a>, <a href="#entry-Utryambakezvara" class="skt-link">Tryambakeśvara</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> soc. nom de lignée [<a href="#entry-gotra" class="skt-link">gotra</a>]; cf. <a href="#entry-Uak_sapaada" class="skt-link">Akṣapāda</a>, <a href="#entry-Ukak_siivaan" class="skt-link">Kakṣīvān</a>, <a href="#entry-Uk_rpa" class="skt-link">Kṛpa</a>, <a href="#entry-Uhaaridrumata" class="skt-link">Hāridrumata</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> bd. not. <a href="#entry-gotra" class="skt-link">gotra</a> de famille du prince <a href="#entry-Usiddhaartha" class="skt-link">Siddhārtha</a>, futur <a href="#entry-Ubuddha" class="skt-link">Buddha</a> <a href="#entry-Uzaakyamuni" class="skt-link">Śākyamuni</a> — <a href="#entry-gautamii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>gautamī</i> myth. np. de Gautamī «(fille) de <a href="#entry-Ugautama" class="skt-link">Gautama</a>», épouse de <a href="#entry-Uka_nva" class="skt-link">Kaṇva</a>, mère adoptive de <a href="#entry-Uzakuntalaa" class="skt-link">Śakuntalā</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> hist. np. de Gautamī_1 Balaśrī, mère de <a href="#entry-Uzaalivaahana" class="skt-link">Śālivāhana</a>.
  </div>
</div>
