    <span class="deva" lang="sa">ग्राभ</span> <i>grābha</i> [<a href="#entry-graaha" class="skt-link">grāha</a>] <a href="#entry-graabha" class="skt-link">m.</a> véd. démon provoquant des maladies.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-graah">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">ग्राह्</span> <i>grāh</i> [ca. <a href="#entry-grah" class="skt-link">grah</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">10</span>] pr. <i>(grāhayati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>grāhita</i><i>)</i> faire prendre, donner, rendre.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-graaha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">ग्राह</span> <i>grāha</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-grah" class="skt-link">grah</a>] a. <a href="#entry-graaha" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-graaha" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-graahii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>grāhī</i> qui saisit, qui capture, qui prend; qui reçoit <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> monstre; rapace; crocodile, requin — <a href="#entry-graaha" class="skt-link">m.</a> capture, saisissement; maladie, paralysie.<br> <i>grāham</i> ind. ifc. afin de prendre ⟨iic.⟩.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-graahaka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">ग्राहक</span> <i>grāhaka</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-grah" class="skt-link">grah</a>] a. <a href="#entry-graahaka" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-graahaka" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-graahikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>grāhikā</i> qui prend, qui capture; qui retient captif <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> qui reçoit; qui achète <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> qui perçoit — ifc. qui saisit — <a href="#entry-graahaka" class="skt-link">m.</a> phil. sujet (qui perçoit) <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> faucon, oiseau de proie <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. épith. de <a href="#entry-Uraahu" class="skt-link">Rāhu</a> l'éclipse.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-graahi">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">ग्राहि</span> <i>grāhi</i> iic. de <a href="#entry-graahin" class="skt-link">grāhin</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-graahin">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">ग्राहिन्</span> <i>grāhin</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-grah" class="skt-link">grah</a>] a. <a href="#entry-graahin" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-graahin" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-graahi_nii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>grāhiṇī</i> ifc. qui prend; qui accepte; qui tient, qui attrape <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> qui obtient; qui garde, qui retient <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> qui scrute, qui épie; qui perçoit <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> qui attire l'attention; qui fascine <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> méd. astringent; constipant.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-graahiphala">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>grāhiphala</i> [<a href="#entry-phala" class="skt-link">phala</a>] <a href="#entry-graahiphala" class="skt-link">n.</a> natu. pomme d'éléphant, «fruit astringent» du <a href="#entry-kapittha" class="skt-link">kapittha</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-graahi" class="skt-link">grāhi</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-graahita">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">ग्राहित</span> <i>grāhita</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-graah" class="skt-link">grāh</a>] a. <a href="#entry-graahita" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-graahita" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-graahitaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>grāhitā</i> qui a été forcé ou invité à prendre.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-graahya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">ग्राह्य</span> <i>grāhya</i> [pfp. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] <a href="#entry-grah" class="skt-link">grah</a>] a. <a href="#entry-graahya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-graahya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-graahyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>grāhyā</i> qui doit être reçu; qui peut être saisi.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-graama">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">ग्राम</span> <i>grāma</i> <a href="#entry-graama" class="skt-link">m.</a> véd. groupe de familles apparentées [<a href="#entry-sajaata" class="skt-link">sajāta</a>], semi-nomade, avec à sa tête un chef [<a href="#entry-graama_nii" class="skt-link">grāmaṇī</a>] responsable devant le chef de la tribu <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> hameau, village; foule — ifc. ensemble des <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> pali <i>gāma</i>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-graama_nii">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>grāmaṇī</i> [<a href="#entry-nii_2" class="skt-link">nī_2</a>] <a href="#entry-graama_nii" class="skt-link">m.</a> chef de village; chef en gén. <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. np. de Grāmaṇī, épith. de <a href="#entry-Uga_neza" class="skt-link">Gaṇeśa</a> «le Chef».
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-graamabaahya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>grāmabāhya</i> [<a href="#entry-baahya" class="skt-link">bāhya</a>] a. <a href="#entry-graamabaahya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-graamabaahya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-graamabaahyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>grāmabāhyā</i> qui habite hors du village; opp. <a href="#entry-graamavaasa" class="skt-link">grāmavāsa</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> var. <i>grāmavāhya</i> mod. id.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-graamadevataa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>grāmadevatā</i> [<a href="#entry-devataa" class="skt-link">devatā</a>] <a href="#entry-graamadevataa" class="skt-link">f.</a> soc. divinité de village; on célèbre son culte à la limite de l'habitat <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> var. <i>grāmadevatī</i> soc. id.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-graamavaasa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>grāmavāsa</i> [<a href="#entry-vaasa_1" class="skt-link">vāsa_1</a>] a. <a href="#entry-graamavaasa" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-graamavaasa" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-graamavaasaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>grāmavāsā</i> qui habite un village; opp. <a href="#entry-graamabaahya" class="skt-link">grāmabāhya</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-graamavaasin">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>grāmavāsin</i> [<a href="#entry--in" class="skt-link">-in</a>] <a href="#entry-graamavaasin" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-graamavaasinii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>grāmavāsinī</i> habitant d'un village.<br> <a href="#entry-graamavaasa" class="skt-link">grāmavāsa</a> <br> <a href="#entry-graama" class="skt-link">grāma</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-graamii_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">ग्रामीण</span> <i>grāmīṇa</i> [<a href="#entry-graama" class="skt-link">grāma</a><a href="#entry--iina" class="skt-link">-īna</a>] a. <a href="#entry-graamii_na" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-graamii_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-graamii_naa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>grāmīṇā</i> <a href="#entry-graamii_nii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>grāmīṇī</i> propre au village; spécifique à un village <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> rustique, grossier, vulgaire — <a href="#entry-graamii_na" class="skt-link">m.</a> villageois, paysan.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-graamika">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">ग्रामिक</span> <i>grāmika</i> [<a href="#entry-graama" class="skt-link">grāma</a><a href="#entry--ika" class="skt-link">-ika</a>] a. <a href="#entry-graamika" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-graamika" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-graamikii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>grāmikī</i> rural, rustique — <a href="#entry-graamika" class="skt-link">m.</a> villageois; chef du village.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-graamin">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">ग्रामिन्</span> <i>grāmin</i> [<a href="#entry-graama" class="skt-link">grāma</a><a href="#entry--in" class="skt-link">-in</a>] a. <a href="#entry-graamin" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-graamin" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-graami_nii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>grāmiṇī</i> rural, rustique — <a href="#entry-graamin" class="skt-link">m.</a> villageois; chef du village.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-graamya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">ग्राम्य</span> <i>grāmya</i> [<a href="#entry-graama" class="skt-link">grāma</a><a href="#entry--ya" class="skt-link">-ya</a>] a. <a href="#entry-graamya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-graamya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-graamyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>grāmyā</i> relatif à un village; produit par un village <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> rustique; vulgaire <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> domestiqué; cultivé — <a href="#entry-graamya" class="skt-link">m.</a> villageois — <a href="#entry-graamya" class="skt-link">n.</a> patois <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> nourriture de la campagne.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-graamyadharma">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>grāmyadharma</i> [<a href="#entry-dharma" class="skt-link">dharma</a>] <a href="#entry-graamyadharma" class="skt-link">m.</a> destin d'un villageois <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> soc. [«droit d'un villageois»] (opp. au devoir d'un reclus) pratique du sexe.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-graamyadharmin">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>grāmyadharmin</i> [<a href="#entry--in" class="skt-link">-in</a>] a. <a href="#entry-graamyadharmin" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-graamyadharmin" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-graamyadharmi_nii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>grāmyadharmiṇī</i> soc. affligé d'addiction sexuelle.<br> <a href="#entry-graamyadharma" class="skt-link">grāmyadharma</a> <br> <a href="#entry-graamya" class="skt-link">grāmya</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-graamyataa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>grāmyatā</i> [<a href="#entry--taa" class="skt-link">-tā</a>] <a href="#entry-graamyataa" class="skt-link">f.</a> rusticité; not. langue vulgaire, patois.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-graamyatva">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>grāmyatva</i> [<a href="#entry--tva" class="skt-link">-tva</a>] <a href="#entry-graamyatva" class="skt-link">n.</a> id.<br> <i>agrāmyatva</i> urbanité.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-graasa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">ग्रास</span> <i>grāsa</i> <a href="#entry-graasa" class="skt-link">m.</a> bouchée, morceau; nourriture.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-graasaambu">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>grāsāmbu</i> [<a href="#entry-ambu" class="skt-link">ambu</a>] <a href="#entry-graasaambu" class="skt-link">n.</a> le manger et le boire.<br> <a href="#entry-graasa" class="skt-link">grāsa</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-graava">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">ग्राव</span> <i>grāva</i> iic. de <a href="#entry-graavan" class="skt-link">grāvan</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-graavan">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">ग्रावन्</span> <i>grāvan</i> <a href="#entry-graavan" class="skt-link">m.</a> pierre; véd. meule à pressurer le <a href="#entry-soma" class="skt-link">soma</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> (contestant le sens précédent) chanteur.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-graavarohaka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>grāvarohaka</i> [<a href="#entry-rohaka" class="skt-link">rohaka</a>] a. <a href="#entry-graavarohaka" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-graavarohaka" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-graavarohakaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>grāvarohakā</i> qui pousse sur les pierres — <a href="#entry-graavarohaka" class="skt-link">m.</a> natu. bot. <i>Physalis flexuosa</i>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-graavastut">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>grāvastut</i> [<a href="#entry-stut" class="skt-link">stut</a>] <a href="#entry-graavastut" class="skt-link">m.</a> soc. «qui fait l'éloge des meules à <a href="#entry-soma" class="skt-link">soma</a>», prêtre assistant du <a href="#entry-hot_r" class="skt-link">hotṛ</a> dans le sacrifice védique.<br> <a href="#entry-graava" class="skt-link">grāva</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-grabh">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">ग्रभ्</span> <i>grabh</i> cf. <a href="#entry-grah" class="skt-link">grah</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-grah">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">ग्रह्</span> <i>grah</i> var. <i>grabh</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">9</span>] pr. <i>(gṛhṇāti)</i> pr. md. <i>(gṛhṇīte)</i> imp. <i>(gṛhāṇa)</i> pft. <i>(jagrāha)</i> aor. [<span class="skt-grammar">3</span>] <i>(ajigrahat)</i> aor. [<span class="skt-grammar">5</span>] <i>(agrahīt)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>gṛhīta</i>, <i>gṛbhīta</i><i>)</i> abs. <i>(</i><i>gṛhītvā</i>, <i>-gṛhya</i><i>)</i> pfp. <i>(</i><i>grāhya</i>, <i>gṛhya</i><i>)</i> inf. <i>(grahītum)</i> pf. <i>(</i><i>anu</i>, <i>upa</i>, <i>ni</i>, <i>pari</i>, <i>pra</i>, <i>prati</i>, <i>vi</i>, <i>sam</i><i>)</i> prendre, saisir, s'emparer de, acquérir; gagner <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> appréhender, comprendre <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> recevoir, accepter <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> éclipser; dérober <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> capturer, faire prisonnier; terrasser — ps. <i>(gṛhyate)</i> être pris, être saisi — ca. <i>(grāhayati)</i> faire prendre, donner, rendre — dés. <i>(jighṛkṣati)</i> cf. <a href="#entry-jigh_rk_s" class="skt-link">jighṛkṣ</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> ang. to grab; all. greifen; fr. agripper, griffe.<br> <i>nāma</i> <i>grah</i> mentionner le nom de.<br> <i>pāṇim</i> <i>grah</i> prendre la main (dans la cérémonie du mariage).<br> <i>idaṃ</i> <i>suvarṇakaṅkaṇaṃ</i> <i>gṛhyatām</i> [<a href="#entry-Uhitopadeza" class="skt-link">Hitopadeśa</a>] Recevez ce bracelet d'or.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-graha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">ग्रह</span> <i>graha</i> [act. <a href="#entry-grah" class="skt-link">grah</a>] <a href="#entry-graha" class="skt-link">m.</a> action de saisir, préhension; prise, capture; confiscation, emprisonnement <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> phil. appréhension (par les sens); compréhension <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> possession, influence magique <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> éclipse — <a href="#entry-graha" class="skt-link">m.</a> crocodile <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> véd. cuiller, louche; not. louche à <a href="#entry-soma" class="skt-link">soma</a>; cuillerée, (contenu d'une) louche <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. génie ravisseur, influence astrale; not. épith. du dragon <a href="#entry-Uraahu" class="skt-link">Rāhu</a>, l'<a href="#entry-asura" class="skt-link">asura</a> éclipseur; cf. <a href="#entry-navagraha" class="skt-link">navagraha</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> astr. planète en gén. <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> phil. [<a href="#entry-tantra" class="skt-link">tantra</a>] possession par un être surnaturel; elle peut affecter tout mâle adulte, sauf les dévots de <a href="#entry-Umahaadeva" class="skt-link">Mahādeva</a> dit-on; cf. <a href="#entry-devagraha" class="skt-link">devagraha</a>, <a href="#entry-pit_rgraha" class="skt-link">pitṛgraha</a>, <a href="#entry-siddhagraha" class="skt-link">siddhagraha</a>, <a href="#entry-raak_sasagraha" class="skt-link">rākṣasagraha</a>, <a href="#entry-gandharvagraha" class="skt-link">gandharvagraha</a>, <a href="#entry-yak_sagraha" class="skt-link">yakṣagraha</a>, <a href="#entry-pizaacagraha" class="skt-link">piśācagraha</a> — ifc. qui saisit, qui s'empare de.<br> <i>grahaṃ</i> <i>gam</i> être fait prisonnier.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-graha_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">ग्रहण</span> <i>grahaṇa</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-grah" class="skt-link">grah</a>] a. <a href="#entry-graha_na" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-graha_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-graha_naa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>grahaṇā</i> qui tient, qui saisit ⟨iic.⟩; qui persiste — <a href="#entry-graha_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> fait de saisir; prise; possession (par un démon); éclipse <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> acceptation; fait d'assumer une forme; prise d'un vêtement <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> phil. (saisie par l'esprit) perception; acquisition de connaissances.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-grahado_sa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>grahadoṣa</i> [<a href="#entry-do_sa" class="skt-link">doṣa</a>] <a href="#entry-grahado_sa" class="skt-link">m.</a> astr. infortune astrale.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-grahalaaghava">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>grahalāghava</i> [<a href="#entry-laaghava" class="skt-link">lāghava</a>] <a href="#entry-grahalaaghava" class="skt-link">n.</a> astr. np. du Grahalāghava, traité sur le mouvement des planètes de <a href="#entry-Uga_neza_1" class="skt-link">Gaṇeśa_1</a> <a href="#entry-Udaivaj_na" class="skt-link">Daivajña</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-grahapati">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>grahapati</i> [<a href="#entry-pati" class="skt-link">pati</a>] <a href="#entry-grahapati" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. de Grahapati, épith. de <a href="#entry-Ucandra" class="skt-link">Candra</a> «Chef des planètes».
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-grahavarman">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>grahavarman</i> [<a href="#entry-varman" class="skt-link">varman</a>] <a href="#entry-grahavarman" class="skt-link">m.</a> hist. np. du roi Grahavarmā <a href="#entry-Umaukhari" class="skt-link">Maukhari</a> de <a href="#entry-Ukanyaakubjaa" class="skt-link">Kanyākubjā</a> (fin du 6⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ siècle); il épousa Rājyaśrī, fille du roi <a href="#entry-Uprabhaakara_1" class="skt-link">Prabhākara_1</a>; il fut tué lors d'une attaque surprise du roi <a href="#entry-Udevagupta" class="skt-link">Devagupta</a> du <a href="#entry-Umaalava" class="skt-link">Mālava</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-grahazaanti">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>grahaśānti</i> [<a href="#entry-zaanti" class="skt-link">śānti</a>] <a href="#entry-grahazaanti" class="skt-link">f.</a> soc. rite de pacification des influences malignes.<br> <a href="#entry-graha" class="skt-link">graha</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-grahiit_r">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">ग्रहीतृ</span> <i>grahītṛ</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-grah" class="skt-link">grah</a>] a. <a href="#entry-grahiit_r" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-grahiit_r" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-grahiitrii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>grahītrī</i> qui prend, qui saisit <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> qui perçoit, qui comprend.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-graiva">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">ग्रैव</span> <i>graiva</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-griivaa" class="skt-link">grīvā</a>] a. <a href="#entry-graiva" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-graiva" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-graivaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>graivā</i> concernant la nuque ou le cou — <a href="#entry-graiva" class="skt-link">n.</a> [«situé à la nuque»] collier.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-graiveya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">ग्रैवेय</span> <i>graiveya</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-griivaa" class="skt-link">grīvā</a><a href="#entry--iiya" class="skt-link">-īya</a>] <a href="#entry-graiveya" class="skt-link">n.</a> [«situé à la nuque»] collier — <a href="#entry-graiveya" class="skt-link">m.</a> chaîne autour du cou d'un éléphant.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-graiveyaka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>graiveyaka</i> [<a href="#entry--ka" class="skt-link">-ka</a>] <a href="#entry-graiveyaka" class="skt-link">n.</a> collier — <a href="#entry-graiveyaka" class="skt-link">m.</a> chaîne autour du cou d'un éléphant <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> pl. jn. classe de 9 divinités formant un collier autour du cou du <a href="#entry-Ulokapuru_sa" class="skt-link">Lokapuruṣa</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-graiveya" class="skt-link">graiveya</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-granth">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">ग्रन्थ्</span> <i>granth</i> var. <i>grath</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">9</span>] pr. <i>(grathnāti)</i> pr. md. <i>(grathnīte)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>grathita</i><i>)</i> joindre, réunir; attacher, lier <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> composer, arranger; écrire — ps. <i>(grathyate)</i> être composé.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-grantha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">ग्रन्थ</span> <i>grantha</i> [act. <a href="#entry-granth" class="skt-link">granth</a>] <a href="#entry-grantha" class="skt-link">m.</a> fait de lier ensemble; collection reliée; nœud <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> œuvre littéraire, texte, livre <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> soc. not. littérature sacrée des Sikhs (Guru Grantha Sahib) <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> écriture [<a href="#entry-lipi" class="skt-link">lipi</a>] utilisée not. au Tibet pour le sanskrit.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-granthaavali">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>granthāvali</i> [<a href="#entry-aavali" class="skt-link">āvali</a>] <a href="#entry-granthaavali" class="skt-link">f.</a> série d'ouvrages.<br> <a href="#entry-grantha" class="skt-link">grantha</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-granthak_rt">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>granthakṛt</i> [<a href="#entry-k_rt_2" class="skt-link">kṛt_2</a>] <a href="#entry-granthak_rt" class="skt-link">m.</a> auteur (d'un ouvrage)
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-granthakaara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>granthakāra</i> [<a href="#entry-kaara" class="skt-link">kāra</a>] <a href="#entry-granthakaara" class="skt-link">m.</a> auteur (d'un ouvrage) <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> var. <i>granthakartṛ</i> id.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-granthamaalaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>granthamālā</i> [<a href="#entry-maalaa" class="skt-link">mālā</a>] <a href="#entry-granthamaalaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> série d'ouvrages.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-granthana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">ग्रन्थन</span> <i>granthana</i> [act. <a href="#entry-granth" class="skt-link">granth</a>] ifc. <a href="#entry-granthana" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-granthanaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>granthanā</i> composition, arrangement, brochage (d'un livre).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-granthastha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>granthastha</i> [<a href="#entry-stha" class="skt-link">stha</a>] a. <a href="#entry-granthastha" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-granthastha" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-granthasthaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>granthasthā</i> se dit d'un savoir lu dans un livre, jugé inférieur à un savoir mémorisé [<a href="#entry-ka_n_thastha" class="skt-link">kaṇṭhastha</a>].
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-granthi">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">ग्रन्थि</span> <i>granthi</i> [act. <a href="#entry-granth" class="skt-link">granth</a>] <a href="#entry-granthi" class="skt-link">m.</a> nœud; jointure, articulation; nœud de tige de bambou <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> nœud du vêtement servant de bourse <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> protubérance, ballot <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> méd. glande <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> nœud serré; difficulté <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> lit. strophe [<a href="#entry-zloka" class="skt-link">śloka</a>] épineuse du <a href="#entry-Umahaabhaarata" class="skt-link">Mah.</a>; on dit que <a href="#entry-Uvyaasa" class="skt-link">Vyāsa</a> en créait dès que <a href="#entry-Uga_neza" class="skt-link">Gaṇeśa</a> menaçait de le rattraper en écrivant l'épopée; il y en a 8800 au total.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-granthika">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">ग्रन्थिक</span> <i>granthika</i> [<a href="#entry-grantha" class="skt-link">grantha</a><a href="#entry--ika" class="skt-link">-ika</a>] <a href="#entry-granthika" class="skt-link">m.</a> narrateur, lettré; astrologue, devin <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. [<a href="#entry-Umahaabhaarata" class="skt-link">Mah.</a>] np. de Granthika «Lettré», fausse identité de <a href="#entry-Unakula" class="skt-link">Nakula</a> déguisé en maître des chevaux [<a href="#entry-suuta_2" class="skt-link">sūta_2</a>], pour se cacher à la cour du roi <a href="#entry-Uviraa_ta" class="skt-link">Virāṭa</a> lors de la 13⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ année d'exil des <a href="#entry-paa_n_davaas" class="skt-link">pāṇḍavās</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-granthin">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">ग्रन्थिन्</span> <i>granthin</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-granth" class="skt-link">granth</a>] a. <a href="#entry-granthin" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-granthin" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-granthinii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>granthinī</i> lettré.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-granthipar_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>granthiparṇa</i> [<a href="#entry-par_na" class="skt-link">parṇa</a>] <a href="#entry-granthipar_na" class="skt-link">m.</a> plante à bulbe.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-granthirasa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>granthirasa</i> [<a href="#entry-rasa" class="skt-link">rasa</a>] <a href="#entry-granthirasa" class="skt-link">m.</a> méd. hormone.<br> <a href="#entry-granthi" class="skt-link">granthi</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-gras">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">ग्रस्</span> <i>gras</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. <i>(grasati)</i> pr. md. <i>(grasate)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>grasta</i><i>)</i> saisir dans sa gueule; avaler, dévorer, engloutir; faire disparaître; éclipser <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> lat. <i>grāmen</i>; all. grasen; ang. grass.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-grasana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">ग्रसन</span> <i>grasana</i> [act. <a href="#entry-gras" class="skt-link">gras</a>] <a href="#entry-grasana" class="skt-link">n.</a> bouche, gueule <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> fait de saisir; fait d'avaler, d'engloutir.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-grasta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">ग्रस्त</span> <i>grasta</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-gras" class="skt-link">gras</a>] a. <a href="#entry-grasta" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-grasta" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-grastaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>grastā</i> dévoré; saisi; éclipsé; possédé (par un démon) — ifc. affecté par; impliqué dans.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-grastacetana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>grastacetana</i> [<a href="#entry-cetana" class="skt-link">cetana</a>] a. <a href="#entry-grastacetana" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-grastacetana" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-grastacetanaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>grastacetanā</i> rendu inconscient.<br> <a href="#entry-grasta" class="skt-link">grasta</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-grathana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">ग्रथन</span> <i>grathana</i> [act. <a href="#entry-granth" class="skt-link">granth</a>] <a href="#entry-grathana" class="skt-link">n.</a> fait de lier, d'attacher — <a href="#entry-grathanaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>grathanā</i> id.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-grii_sma">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">ग्रीष्म</span> <i>grīṣma</i> [<a href="#entry-gras" class="skt-link">gras</a>] <a href="#entry-grii_sma" class="skt-link">m.</a> la saison [<a href="#entry-_rtu" class="skt-link">ṛtu</a>] chaude, été (mois de <a href="#entry-jyai_s_tha" class="skt-link">jyaiṣṭha</a> et <a href="#entry-aa_saa_dha" class="skt-link">āṣāḍha</a>).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-griivaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">ग्रीवा</span> <i>grīvā</i> <a href="#entry-griivaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> cou, nuque.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-gu">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">गु</span> <i>gu</i> ifc. de <a href="#entry-go" class="skt-link">go</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-gu_da">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">गुड</span> <i>guḍa</i> <a href="#entry-gu_da" class="skt-link">m.</a> boule, balle <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> mélasse (de jus de canne); sucre brun (de palme) — <a href="#entry-gu_daa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>guḍā</i> boule, boulette; pilule.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-gu_daakeza">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>guḍākeśa</i> [<a href="#entry-keza" class="skt-link">keśa</a>] <a href="#entry-gu_daakeza" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. [BhG.] np. de Guḍākeśa, épith. d'<a href="#entry-Uarjuna" class="skt-link">Arjuna</a> «Aux cheveux épais».<br> <a href="#entry-gu_da" class="skt-link">guḍa</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-gu_n_da">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">गुण्ड</span> <i>guṇḍa</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-gu_n_th" class="skt-link">guṇṭh</a>] <a href="#entry-gu_n_da" class="skt-link">m.</a> soc. capsule de protection, not. du <a href="#entry-lifga" class="skt-link">liṅga</a> portatif de la secte <a href="#entry-viirazaiva" class="skt-link">vīraśaiva</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-gu_n_dicaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">गुण्डिचा</span> <i>guṇḍicā</i> <a href="#entry-gu_n_dicaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> géo. np. du temple de Guṇḍicā à <a href="#entry-Upurii" class="skt-link">Purī</a>, résidence d'été de <a href="#entry-Ujagannaatha" class="skt-link">Jagannātha</a> pour 9 jours lors de la grande procession [<a href="#entry-rathayaatraa" class="skt-link">rathayātrā</a>], symbolisant le retour de <a href="#entry-Uk_r_s_na" class="skt-link">Kṛṣṇa</a> à <a href="#entry-Uv_rndaavana" class="skt-link">Vṛndāvana</a>; il est nommé d'après l'épouse d'<a href="#entry-Uindradyumna" class="skt-link">Indradyumna</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-gu_n_th">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">गुण्ठ्</span> <i>guṇṭh</i> var. <i>guṇḍ</i> ca. <i>(guṇṭhayati)</i> pf. <i>(</i><i>ava</i><i>)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>guṇṭhita</i><i>)</i> couvrir, envelopper.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-gu_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">गुण</span> <i>guṇa</i> <a href="#entry-gu_na" class="skt-link">m.</a> fil; corde (d'un arc, d'un instrument de musique) <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> qualité constitutive, propriété, attribut <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> subdivision, sorte, catégorie, espèce <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> mérite; grande qualité, excellence <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> lit. qualité littéraire; facteur de beauté poétique [<a href="#entry-kaavyazobhaa" class="skt-link">kāvyaśobhā</a>] <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> phon. augmentation d'une voyelle au 1⟨sup⟩er⟨/sup⟩ degré: le <a href="#entry-gu_na" class="skt-link">guṇa</a> de (‘<i>a</i>’, ‘<i>i</i>’, ‘<i>u</i>’, ‘<i>ṛ</i>’, ‘<i>ḷ</i>’) est resp. (‘<i>a</i>’, ‘<i>e</i>’, ‘<i>o</i>’, ‘<i>ar</i>’, ‘<i>al</i>’) <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> phil. [<a href="#entry-saafkhya" class="skt-link">sāṃkhya</a>] qualité caractérisant l'une des trois essences de la nature: le Bien ou Pure Essence de l'Être <a href="#entry-sattva" class="skt-link">sattva</a> (pureté, vérité), la Passion <a href="#entry-rajas" class="skt-link">rajas</a> (force, désir), et la Ténèbre <a href="#entry-tamas" class="skt-link">tamas</a> (ignorance, inertie); ces trois qualités s'équilibrent dans les choses, dont on caractérise la nature par leurs rapports respectifs <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> phil. [<a href="#entry-vaize_sika" class="skt-link">vaiśeṣika</a>] la catégorie [<a href="#entry-padaartha" class="skt-link">padārtha</a>] des propriétés; <a href="#entry-Uka_naada" class="skt-link">Kaṇāda</a> en dénombre 17: <a href="#entry-ruupa" class="skt-link">rūpa</a> l'aspect, <a href="#entry-rasa" class="skt-link">rasa</a> la saveur, <a href="#entry-gandha" class="skt-link">gandha</a> l'odeur, <a href="#entry-sparza" class="skt-link">sparśa</a> le toucher, <a href="#entry-safkhyaa_2" class="skt-link">saṃkhyā_2</a> le nombre, <a href="#entry-parimaa_na" class="skt-link">parimāṇa</a> la dimension <a href="#entry-p_rthaktva" class="skt-link">pṛthaktva</a> la singularité, <a href="#entry-prayatna" class="skt-link">prayatna</a> la volition, <a href="#entry-buddhayas" class="skt-link">buddhayas</a> les perceptions, et 4 paires de contraires [<a href="#entry-dvandva" class="skt-link">dvandva</a>]: <a href="#entry-sa_myogavibhaagau" class="skt-link">saṃyogavibhāgau</a> le contact et la séparation, <a href="#entry-paratvaaparatve" class="skt-link">paratvāparatve</a> l'éloignement et la proximité, <a href="#entry-sukhadu_hkhe" class="skt-link">sukhaduḥkhe</a> le plaisir et la peine, <a href="#entry-icchaadve_sau" class="skt-link">icchādveṣau</a> le désir et l'aversion; le <a href="#entry-Utarkasafgraha" class="skt-link">Tarkasaṃgraha</a> en compte 24, en ajoutant: <a href="#entry-gurutva" class="skt-link">gurutva</a> la pesanteur, <a href="#entry-dravatva" class="skt-link">dravatva</a> la fluidité, <a href="#entry-sneha" class="skt-link">sneha</a> la viscosité, <a href="#entry-zabda" class="skt-link">śabda</a> le son, <a href="#entry-dharma" class="skt-link">dharma</a> le bien, <a href="#entry-adharma" class="skt-link">adharma</a> le mal, et <a href="#entry-sa_mskaara" class="skt-link">saṃskāra</a> la disposition <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> math. coefficient multiplicateur — ifc. forme des multiples: <a href="#entry-trigu_na" class="skt-link">triguṇa</a> le triple.<br> <i>guṇībhū</i> être subordonné à ⟨g.⟩.<br> <i>guṇībhāva</i> subordonné (opp. <i>pradhānabhāva</i> principal).<br> <i>guṇībhūta</i> subordonné à ⟨g.⟩; secondaire <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> investi avec des attributs.<br> <i>guṇībhūtavyañgya</i> lit. [<a href="#entry-Uaanandavardhana" class="skt-link">Ānandavardhana</a>] dont le sens suggéré est secondaire (se dit de poésie [<a href="#entry-kaavya" class="skt-link">kāvya</a>] qui n'a pas le développement d'une émotion [<a href="#entry-rasa" class="skt-link">rasa</a>] comme but principal); opp. <a href="#entry-dhvani" class="skt-link">dhvani</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-gu_naa_dhya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>guṇāḍhya</i> [<a href="#entry-aa_dhya" class="skt-link">āḍhya</a>] <a href="#entry-gu_naa_dhya" class="skt-link">m.</a> hist. np. de Guṇāḍhya «Riche en qualités», poète du 4⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ ou 5⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ siècle; on dit qu'ayant perdu un pari avec le grammairien <a href="#entry-Usarvavarmaa" class="skt-link">Sarvavarmā</a>, il abjura le sanskrit et écrivit la <a href="#entry-Ub_rhatkathaa" class="skt-link">Bṛhatkathā</a> en prakrit.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-gu_nabhadra">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>guṇabhadra</i> [<a href="#entry-bhadra" class="skt-link">bhadra</a>] <a href="#entry-gu_nabhadra" class="skt-link">m.</a> hist. np. du maître jaïne <a href="#entry-aacaarya" class="skt-link">ācārya</a> Guṇabhadra (10⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ siècle), disciple de <a href="#entry-Ujinasena" class="skt-link">Jinasena</a>; il compléta son <a href="#entry-Uaadipuraa_na_2" class="skt-link">Ādipurāṇa_2</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-gu_nado_sa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>guṇadoṣa</i> [<a href="#entry-do_sa" class="skt-link">doṣa</a>] <a href="#entry-gu_nado_sa" class="skt-link">m.</a> du. la vertu et le vice — <a href="#entry-gu_nado_sa" class="skt-link">n.</a> sg. id.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-gu_naga_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>guṇagaṇa</i> [<a href="#entry-ga_na" class="skt-link">gaṇa</a>] <a href="#entry-gu_naga_na" class="skt-link">m.</a> nombre ou suite de vertus.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-gu_nagaa_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>guṇagāṇa</i> [<a href="#entry-gaa_na" class="skt-link">gāṇa</a>] <a href="#entry-gu_nagaa_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> panégyrique.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-gu_nagraha_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>guṇagrahaṇa</i> [<a href="#entry-graha_na" class="skt-link">grahaṇa</a>] <a href="#entry-gu_nagraha_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> fait d'apprécier le mérite.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-gu_nakezii">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>guṇakeśī</i> [<a href="#entry-kezii" class="skt-link">keśī</a>] <a href="#entry-gu_nakezii" class="skt-link">f.</a> myth. [<a href="#entry-Umahaabhaarata" class="skt-link">Mah.</a>] np. de la belle Guṇakeśī «aux beaux cheveux», fille de <a href="#entry-Umaatali" class="skt-link">Mātali</a>; elle épousa le <a href="#entry-naaga" class="skt-link">nāga</a> <a href="#entry-Usumukha" class="skt-link">Sumukha</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-gu_nakira_naavaliiprakaaza">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>guṇakiraṇāvalīprakāśa</i> [<a href="#entry-kira_naavaliiprakaaza" class="skt-link">kiraṇāvalīprakāśa</a>] iic. phil. [<a href="#entry-nyaaya" class="skt-link">nyāya</a>] qualité du <a href="#entry-Ukira_naavaliiprakaaza" class="skt-link">Kiraṇāvalīprakāśa</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-gu_nakira_naavaliiprakaazadiidhiti">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>guṇakiraṇāvalīprakāśadīdhiti</i> [<a href="#entry-diidhiti" class="skt-link">dīdhiti</a>] <a href="#entry-gu_nakira_naavaliiprakaazadiidhiti" class="skt-link">f.</a> lit. [<a href="#entry-nyaaya" class="skt-link">nyāya</a>] np. de la Guṇakiraṇāvalīprakāśadīdhiti «Éloge des qualités de l'Éclat du Recueil de rayons», œuvre de logique de <a href="#entry-Uraghunaatha_1" class="skt-link">Raghunātha_1</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-gu_nakira_naavaliiprakaaza" class="skt-link">guṇakiraṇāvalīprakāśa</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-gu_namaya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>guṇamaya</i> [<a href="#entry-maya" class="skt-link">maya</a>] a. <a href="#entry-gu_namaya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-gu_namaya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-gu_namayii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>guṇamayī</i> en fil <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> doué de qualités, méritant.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-gu_nasamanvita">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>guṇasamanvita</i> [<a href="#entry-samanvita" class="skt-link">samanvita</a>] a. <a href="#entry-gu_nasamanvita" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-gu_nasamanvita" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-gu_nasamanvitaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>guṇasamanvitā</i> doué de vertus.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-gu_natas">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>guṇatas</i> [<a href="#entry--tas" class="skt-link">-tas</a>] adv. selon ses qualités; selon ses mérites <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> phil. selon la proportion des trois essences.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-gu_nav_rtti">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>guṇavṛtti</i> [<a href="#entry-v_rtti" class="skt-link">vṛtti</a>] <a href="#entry-gu_nav_rtti" class="skt-link">f.</a> phil. condition secondaire; opp. <a href="#entry-mukhyaa" class="skt-link">mukhyā</a> <a href="#entry-v_rtti" class="skt-link">vṛtti</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> gram. sens figuré d'un mot (opp. sens littéral [<a href="#entry-abhidhaa_2" class="skt-link">abhidhā_2</a>], sens suggéré [<a href="#entry-vya_njanaa" class="skt-link">vyañjanā</a>]); sens métaphorique [<a href="#entry-upacaara" class="skt-link">upacāra</a>]; connotation; sens technique spécialisé [<a href="#entry-laak_sa_na" class="skt-link">lākṣaṇa</a>].
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-gu_navacana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>guṇavacana</i> [<a href="#entry-vacana" class="skt-link">vacana</a>] <a href="#entry-gu_navacana" class="skt-link">n.</a> gram. [«mot exprimant une qualité»] attribut, adjectif — a. <a href="#entry-gu_navacana" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-gu_navacana" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-gu_navacanaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>guṇavacanā</i> qui dénote un qualité; utilisé comme adjectif.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-gu_navat">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">गुणवत्</span> <i>guṇavat</i> [<a href="#entry-gu_na" class="skt-link">guṇa</a><a href="#entry--vat" class="skt-link">-vat</a>] a. <a href="#entry-gu_navat" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-gu_navat" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-gu_navatii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>guṇavatī</i> méritant, vertueux, qui a des qualités <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> muni d'une corde.<br> <i>dravyam</i> <i>eva</i> <i>guṇavat</i> seules les substances possèdent des qualités.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-gu_navrata">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>guṇavrata</i> [<a href="#entry-vrata" class="skt-link">vrata</a>] <a href="#entry-gu_navrata" class="skt-link">n.</a> jn. vœux méritoire, observance rituelle <a href="#entry-jaina" class="skt-link">jaina</a>; on en compte trois: activité limitée [<a href="#entry-digvrata" class="skt-link">digvrata</a>], consommation limitée [<a href="#entry-bhogopabhogavrata" class="skt-link">bhogopabhogavrata</a>] et absence de péché gratuit [<a href="#entry-anarthada_n_davrata" class="skt-link">anarthadaṇḍavrata</a>].
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-gu_nazabda">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>guṇaśabda</i> [<a href="#entry-zabda" class="skt-link">śabda</a>] <a href="#entry-gu_nazabda" class="skt-link">m.</a> gram. mot dénotant une qualité; adjectif.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-gu_nin">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">गुणिन्</span> <i>guṇin</i> [<a href="#entry-gu_na" class="skt-link">guṇa</a><a href="#entry--in" class="skt-link">-in</a>] a. <a href="#entry-gu_nin" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-gu_nin" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-gu_ninii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>guṇinī</i> méritant, vertueux, doué de qualités <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> muni d'une corde.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-gu_nita">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">गुणित</span> <i>guṇita</i> [<a href="#entry-gu_na" class="skt-link">guṇa</a><a href="#entry--ita" class="skt-link">-ita</a>] a. <a href="#entry-gu_nita" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-gu_nita" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-gu_nitaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>guṇitā</i> multiplié (avec ⟨i. iic.⟩) <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> augmenté; intensifié; pratiqué fréquemment <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> connecté avec ⟨iic.⟩.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-gu_nopeta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>guṇopeta</i> [<a href="#entry-upeta" class="skt-link">upeta</a>] a. <a href="#entry-gu_nopeta" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-gu_nopeta" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-gu_nopetaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>guṇopetā</i> pourvu de qualités.<br> <a href="#entry-gu_na" class="skt-link">guṇa</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-gu_nya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">गुण्य</span> <i>guṇya</i> [<a href="#entry-gu_na" class="skt-link">guṇa</a><a href="#entry--ya" class="skt-link">-ya</a>] a. <a href="#entry-gu_nya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-gu_nya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-gu_nyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>guṇyā</i> pourvu de qualités ou de vertus <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> à énumérer <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> selon la qualité <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> math. multiplicande.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-guccha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">गुच्छ</span> <i>guccha</i> var. <i>gutsa</i> <a href="#entry-guccha" class="skt-link">m.</a> buisson; bosquet <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> bouquet, gerbe; botte, brassée <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> collier (de perles).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-gucchaka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>gucchaka</i> [<a href="#entry--ka" class="skt-link">-ka</a>] <a href="#entry-gucchaka" class="skt-link">m.</a> id. <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> lit. collection d'ouvrages.<br> <a href="#entry-guccha" class="skt-link">guccha</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-guda">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">गुद</span> <i>guda</i> <a href="#entry-guda" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-guda" class="skt-link">n.</a> intestin, entrailles; anus — <a href="#entry-gudaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>gudā</i> ifc. id. <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> pl. intestins, viscères.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-guggulu">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">गुग्गुलु</span> <i>guggulu</i> var. <i>gulgulu</i> <a href="#entry-guggulu" class="skt-link">n.</a> natu. résine du balsamier bot. <i>Commiphora mukul</i>; myrrhe indienne, utilisée comme parfum.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-guh">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">गुह्</span> <i>guh</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. <i>(gūhati)</i> pft. <i>(jugūha)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>gūḍha</i><i>)</i> pfp. <i>(</i><i>gohya</i>, <i>guhya</i><i>)</i> pf. <i>(</i><i>ni</i><i>)</i> couvrir <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> cacher, dissimuler — ca. <i>(gūhayati)</i> cacher, dissimuler.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-guha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">गुह</span> <i>guha</i> [obj. <a href="#entry-guh" class="skt-link">guh</a>] <a href="#entry-guha" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. de Guha, épith. de <a href="#entry-Uskanda" class="skt-link">Skanda</a> «élevé en cachette» <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. [<a href="#entry-Uraamaaya_na" class="skt-link">Rām.</a>] np. de Guha_1, roi <a href="#entry-ni_saada" class="skt-link">niṣāda</a>; sa capitale était <a href="#entry-Uz_rfgavera" class="skt-link">Śṛṅgavera</a>, sur les bords de la <a href="#entry-Ugafgaa" class="skt-link">Gaṅgā</a>; il faisait traverser le fleuve aux héros; <a href="#entry-Uraama" class="skt-link">Rāma</a> était son ami, et l'embrassait (en dépit de sa caste) — <a href="#entry-guhaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>guhā</i> cachette, caverne — adv. <i>guhā</i> en cachette, secrètement; opp. <a href="#entry-aavis" class="skt-link">āvis</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-guhya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">गुह्य</span> <i>guhya</i> [pfp. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] <a href="#entry-guh" class="skt-link">guh</a>] a. <a href="#entry-guhya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-guhya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-guhyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>guhyā</i> qui doit être caché, secret, mystérieux — <a href="#entry-guhya" class="skt-link">n.</a> secret, mystère, formule magique — <a href="#entry-guhya" class="skt-link">m.</a> organe sexuel.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-guhyaka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">गुह्यक</span> <i>guhyaka</i> [<a href="#entry-guhya" class="skt-link">guhya</a><a href="#entry--ka" class="skt-link">-ka</a>] <a href="#entry-guhyaka" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. esprit caché, génie gardien des trésors de <a href="#entry-Ukubera" class="skt-link">Kubera</a>; le chef des <i>guhyakās</i> est <a href="#entry-Urevanta" class="skt-link">Revanta</a>; cf. <a href="#entry-Unalakuuvara" class="skt-link">Nalakūvara</a>, <a href="#entry-Uma_nigriiva" class="skt-link">Maṇigrīva</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-guhyasamaaja">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>guhyasamāja</i> [<a href="#entry-samaaja" class="skt-link">samāja</a>] <a href="#entry-guhyasamaaja" class="skt-link">m.</a> bd. np. du Guhyasamāja, communauté ésotérique, école d'interprétation du bd. tantr. <a href="#entry-Uvajraayana" class="skt-link">Vajrāyana</a>; cf. <a href="#entry-Uaaryadeva" class="skt-link">Āryadeva</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-guhyasamaajatantra">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>guhyasamājatantra</i> [<a href="#entry-tantra" class="skt-link">tantra</a>] <a href="#entry-guhyasamaajatantra" class="skt-link">n.</a> bd. np. du Guhyasamājatantra, enseignement du système <a href="#entry-Uguhyasamaaja" class="skt-link">Guhyasamāja</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-guhyasamaaja" class="skt-link">guhyasamāja</a> <br> <a href="#entry-guhya" class="skt-link">guhya</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-guhyatama">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">गुह्यतम</span> <i>guhyatama</i> [super. <a href="#entry-guhya" class="skt-link">guhya</a>] a. <a href="#entry-guhyatama" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-guhyatama" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-guhyatamaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>guhyatamā</i> le plus secret.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-guhyatara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">गुह्यतर</span> <i>guhyatara</i> [compar. <a href="#entry-guhya" class="skt-link">guhya</a>] a. <a href="#entry-guhyatara" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-guhyatara" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-guhyataraa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>guhyatarā</i> plus secret.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-gulma">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">गुल्म</span> <i>gulma</i> <a href="#entry-gulma" class="skt-link">m.</a> buisson, bosquet, bouquet d'arbres <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> section, unité d'armée comprenant trois troupes [<a href="#entry-senaamukha" class="skt-link">senāmukha</a>]; cf. <a href="#entry-ga_na" class="skt-link">gaṇa</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-gulmasthaana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>gulmasthāna</i> [<a href="#entry-sthaana" class="skt-link">sthāna</a>] <a href="#entry-gulmasthaana" class="skt-link">n.</a> soc. poste de police.<br> <a href="#entry-gulma" class="skt-link">gulma</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-gulpha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">गुल्फ</span> <i>gulpha</i> var. <i>kulpha</i> <a href="#entry-gulpha" class="skt-link">m.</a> cheville.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-gulphadaghna">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>gulphadaghna</i> [<a href="#entry-daghna" class="skt-link">daghna</a>] a. <a href="#entry-gulphadaghna" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-gulphadaghna" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-gulphadaghnaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>gulphadaghnā</i> <a href="#entry-gulphadaghnii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>gulphadaghnī</i> qui a (de l'eau) jusqu'aux chevilles.<br> <a href="#entry-gulpha" class="skt-link">gulpha</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-gumph">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">गुम्फ्</span> <i>gumph</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">6</span>] pr. <i>(gumphati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>gumphita</i><i>)</i> enfiler, assembler en collier ou en guirlande.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-gumpha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">गुम्फ</span> <i>gumpha</i> [act. <a href="#entry-gumph" class="skt-link">gumph</a>] <a href="#entry-gumpha" class="skt-link">m.</a> mise en enfilade; assemblage en guirlande; bracelet.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-gumphita">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">गुम्फित</span> <i>gumphita</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-gumph" class="skt-link">gumph</a>] a. <a href="#entry-gumphita" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-gumphita" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-gumphitaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>gumphitā</i> arrangé en ordre, aligné.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-gup">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">गुप्</span> <i>gup</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">10</span>] pr. <i>(gopayati)</i> pft. <i>(jugopa)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>gupta</i><i>)</i> garder, protéger, défendre — dés. md. <i>(jugupsate)</i> se défendre de ⟨abl.⟩; éprouver du dégoût pour <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> être sur ses gardes; se méfier de, éviter, détester, mépriser ⟨acc.⟩.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-gupta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">गुप्त</span> <i>gupta</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-gup" class="skt-link">gup</a>] a. <a href="#entry-gupta" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-gupta" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-guptaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>guptā</i> protégé, défendu; caché, secret — <a href="#entry-gupta" class="skt-link">m.</a> hist. premier empereur de la dynastie <a href="#entry-Ugupta" class="skt-link">Gupta</a>, ou célèbre [<a href="#entry-zrii" class="skt-link">śrī</a>] roi Gupta, qui fonda la dynastie [<a href="#entry-sa_mvat" class="skt-link">saṃvat</a>] du même nom, sous le titre <a href="#entry-Ucandragupta_1" class="skt-link">Candragupta_1</a> en 320; il régnait sur une large partie de la vallée du Gange; son fils <a href="#entry-Usamudragupta" class="skt-link">Samudragupta</a> lui succéda — ifc. soc. forme des np. <a href="#entry-vaizya" class="skt-link">vaiśya</a>: «protecteur des ...».
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-guptacara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>guptacara</i> [<a href="#entry-cara" class="skt-link">cara</a>] <a href="#entry-guptacara" class="skt-link">m.</a> espion, agent secret.<br> <a href="#entry-gupta" class="skt-link">gupta</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-gupti">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">गुप्ति</span> <i>gupti</i> [act. <a href="#entry-gup" class="skt-link">gup</a>] <a href="#entry-gupti" class="skt-link">f.</a> protection, préservation, fait de cacher <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> défense militaire <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> rempart; prison.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-gur">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">गुर्</span> <i>gur</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">6</span>] pr. md. <i>(gurate)</i> lever, soulever; peser.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-gurjara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">गुर्जर</span> <i>gurjara</i> <a href="#entry-gurjara" class="skt-link">m.</a> géo. le pays Gurjara, mod. Gujarāt; cf. <a href="#entry-Udvaarakaa" class="skt-link">Dvārakā</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> pl. <i>gurjarās</i> ses habitants.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-gurjaradeza">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>gurjaradeśa</i> [<a href="#entry-deza" class="skt-link">deśa</a>] <a href="#entry-gurjaradeza" class="skt-link">m.</a> géo. le pays des <a href="#entry-gurjaraas" class="skt-link">gurjarās</a>, mod. Gujarāt.<br> <a href="#entry-gurjara" class="skt-link">gurjara</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-guru">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">गुरु</span> <i>guru</i> [<a href="#entry-gur" class="skt-link">gur</a>] a. <a href="#entry-guru" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-guru" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-gurvii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>gurvī</i> lourd, grave, respectable, vénérable; grand, important <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> phon. long (voyelle ou syllabe); opp. <a href="#entry-laghu" class="skt-link">laghu</a> — <a href="#entry-guru" class="skt-link">m.</a> précepteur, guide spirituel, maître; guide divin; Dieu incarné <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. np. de Guru, abréviation de <a href="#entry-Usuraguru" class="skt-link">Suraguru</a> «Précepteur (des dieux)», épith. de <a href="#entry-Ub_rhaspati" class="skt-link">Bṛhaspati</a>; astr. il personnifie la planète [<a href="#entry-graha" class="skt-link">graha</a>] Jupiter <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> pl. <i>guravas</i> (adresse respectueuse) Maître, Vénérable <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> lat. <i>gravis</i>; fr. grave.<br> <i>gururbrahmā</i> <i>gururviṣṇuḥ</i> <i>gururdevo</i> <i>maheśvaraḥ</i> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> <i>gurussākṣāt</i> <i>paraṃ</i> <i>brahma</i> <i>tasmai</i> <i>śrī</i> <i>gurave</i> <i>namaḥ</i> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> [Śrīgurustotra] Le Maître est <a href="#entry-Ubrahmaa" class="skt-link">Brahmā</a>, le Maître est <a href="#entry-Uvi_s_nu" class="skt-link">Viṣṇu</a>, le Divin Maître est <a href="#entry-Uziva" class="skt-link">Śiva</a>; en vérité le Maître est Dieu: révérons le Maître.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-gurubandhu">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>gurubandhu</i> [<a href="#entry-bandhu" class="skt-link">bandhu</a>] a. <a href="#entry-gurubandhu" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-gurubandhu" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-gurubandhu" class="skt-link">f.</a> qui a le même maître que — <a href="#entry-gurubandhu" class="skt-link">m.</a> compagnon d'études, camarade.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-gurudak_si_naa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>gurudakṣiṇā</i> [<a href="#entry-dak_si_naa" class="skt-link">dakṣiṇā</a>] <a href="#entry-gurudak_si_naa" class="skt-link">f.</a> soc. honoraires payés par l'élève à son maître à la fin de ses études.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-gurudatta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>gurudatta</i> [<a href="#entry-datta" class="skt-link">datta</a>] a. <a href="#entry-gurudatta" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-gurudatta" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-gurudattaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>gurudattā</i> donné par le maître.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-gurudattamantra">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>gurudattamantra</i> [<a href="#entry-mantra" class="skt-link">mantra</a>] <a href="#entry-gurudattamantra" class="skt-link">n.</a> soc. <a href="#entry-mantra" class="skt-link">mantra</a> donné par le maître.<br> <a href="#entry-gurudatta" class="skt-link">gurudatta</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-gurudeva">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>gurudeva</i> [<a href="#entry-deva" class="skt-link">deva</a>] <a href="#entry-gurudeva" class="skt-link">m.</a> phil. maître vénéré comme dieu vivant.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-gurudvaadazii">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>gurudvādaśī</i> [<a href="#entry-dvaadazii" class="skt-link">dvādaśī</a>] <a href="#entry-gurudvaadazii" class="skt-link">f.</a> soc. festival en l'honneur de <a href="#entry-Udattaatreya" class="skt-link">Dattātreya</a>, le 12⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ jour de la quinzaine sombre [<a href="#entry-k_r_s_napak_sa" class="skt-link">kṛṣṇapakṣa</a>] du mois <a href="#entry-kaarttika" class="skt-link">kārttika</a>; on y fête aussi <a href="#entry-Uvallabhaacaarya" class="skt-link">Vallabhācārya</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-gurugiitaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>gurugītā</i> [<a href="#entry-giitaa" class="skt-link">gītā</a>] <a href="#entry-gurugiitaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> lit. np. de la Gurugītā «Louange au Maître», dialogue entre <a href="#entry-Upaarvatii" class="skt-link">Pārvatī</a> et <a href="#entry-Uziva" class="skt-link">Śiva</a> enseignant comment révérer son <a href="#entry-guru" class="skt-link">guru</a>; c'est une annexe du SkP.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-gurugraama">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>gurugrāma</i> [<a href="#entry-graama" class="skt-link">grāma</a>] <a href="#entry-gurugraama" class="skt-link">m.</a> géo. np. de la ville de Gurugrāma «Village du maître (<a href="#entry-Udro_na" class="skt-link">Droṇa</a>)»; mod. Gurgaon au <a href="#entry-Uhariyaa_na" class="skt-link">Hariyāṇa</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-gurukula">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>gurukula</i> [<a href="#entry-kula" class="skt-link">kula</a>] <a href="#entry-gurukula" class="skt-link">n.</a> soc. maison du maître, ermitage; y vivent sa famille et les pensionnaires de son école <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> mod. école d'enseignement traditionnel du sanskrit.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-gurulaaghava">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>gurulāghava</i> [<a href="#entry-laaghava" class="skt-link">lāghava</a>] <a href="#entry-gurulaaghava" class="skt-link">n.</a> importance relative; le pour et le contre [«le lourd et le léger»].
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-gurumukha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>gurumukha</i> [<a href="#entry-mukha" class="skt-link">mukha</a>] a. <a href="#entry-gurumukha" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-gurumukha" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-gurumukhii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>gurumukhī</i> de la bouche du maître — <a href="#entry-gurumukhii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>gurumukhī</i> hist. écriture <i>gurmukhī</i> utilisée au Pañjab à partir du 14⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ siècle, évoluée de la <a href="#entry-devanaagarii" class="skt-link">devanāgarī</a>; elle fut utilisée not. pour le Livre sacré des Sikhs (Guru <a href="#entry-Ugrantha" class="skt-link">Grantha</a> Sahib).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-gurumukhopadeza">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>gurumukhopadeśa</i> [<a href="#entry-upadeza" class="skt-link">upadeśa</a>] <a href="#entry-gurumukhopadeza" class="skt-link">m.</a> soc. initiation par le maître.<br> <a href="#entry-gurumukha" class="skt-link">gurumukha</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-gurupa_ncamii">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>gurupañcamī</i> [<a href="#entry-pa_ncamii" class="skt-link">pañcamī</a>] <a href="#entry-gurupa_ncamii" class="skt-link">f.</a> cf. <a href="#entry-zrutapa_ncamii" class="skt-link">śrutapañcamī</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-gurupuur_nimaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>gurupūrṇimā</i> [<a href="#entry-puur_nimaa" class="skt-link">pūrṇimā</a>] <a href="#entry-gurupuur_nimaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> jour de pleine lune du mois <a href="#entry-aa_saa_dha" class="skt-link">āṣāḍha</a>, syn. <a href="#entry-aa_saa_dhii" class="skt-link">āṣāḍhī</a>; soc. on y célèbre le sage <a href="#entry-Uvyaasa" class="skt-link">Vyāsa</a>, et par extension son précepteur spirituel; il marque le retour des pluies, et le début des exercices spirituels [<a href="#entry-saadhana" class="skt-link">sādhana</a>].
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-gurutaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">गुरुता</span> <i>gurutā</i> [<a href="#entry-guru" class="skt-link">guru</a><a href="#entry--taa" class="skt-link">-tā</a>] <a href="#entry-gurutaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> poids <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> importance; respectabilité, dignité <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> soc. qualification d'un précepteur spirituel.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-gurutattva">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>gurutattva</i> [<a href="#entry-tattva" class="skt-link">tattva</a>] <a href="#entry-gurutattva" class="skt-link">n.</a> phil. principe de guide divin.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-gurutva">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">गुरुत्व</span> <i>gurutva</i> [<a href="#entry-guru" class="skt-link">guru</a><a href="#entry--tva" class="skt-link">-tva</a>] <a href="#entry-gurutva" class="skt-link">n.</a> poids <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> mus. longueur d'une voyelle <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> fardeau, ennui <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> méd. sévérité (traitement) <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> grandeur, magnitude <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> respectabilité, dignité <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> soc. qualification d'un précepteur spirituel.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-guruvaara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>guruvāra</i> [<a href="#entry-vaara" class="skt-link">vāra</a>] <a href="#entry-guruvaara" class="skt-link">m.</a> jeudi, le jour de Jupiter [<a href="#entry-Ub_rhaspati" class="skt-link">Bṛhaspati</a>]; il est sacré à <a href="#entry-Ulak_smii" class="skt-link">Lakṣmī</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-guruvat">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>guruvat</i> [<a href="#entry--vat" class="skt-link">-vat</a>] adv. comme un maître <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> comme pour un maître.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-guruzi_sya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>guruśiṣya</i> [<a href="#entry-zi_sya" class="skt-link">śiṣya</a>] a. <a href="#entry-guruzi_sya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-guruzi_sya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-guruzi_syaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>guruśiṣyā</i> soc. qui a trait aux rapports entre le maître et l'élève.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-guruzi_syasa_mvaada">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>guruśiṣyasaṃvāda</i> [<a href="#entry-sa_mvaada" class="skt-link">saṃvāda</a>] <a href="#entry-guruzi_syasa_mvaada" class="skt-link">m.</a> lit. np. du Guruśiṣyasaṃvāda «Dialogue entre le maître et l'élève», dialogue philosophique dû à <a href="#entry-Ucara_nadaasa" class="skt-link">Caraṇadāsa</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-guruzi_syasambandha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>guruśiṣyasambandha</i> [<a href="#entry-sambandha" class="skt-link">saṃbandha</a>] <a href="#entry-guruzi_syasambandha" class="skt-link">m.</a> soc. lien entre le maître et l'élève.<br> <a href="#entry-guruzi_sya" class="skt-link">guruśiṣya</a> <br> <a href="#entry-guru" class="skt-link">guru</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-guu_dha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">गूढ</span> <i>gūḍha</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-guh" class="skt-link">guh</a>] a. <a href="#entry-guu_dha" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-guu_dha" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-guu_dhaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>gūḍhā</i> caché, secret — <a href="#entry-guu_dha" class="skt-link">n.</a> lieu secret, cachette.<br> <i>tama</i> <i>āsīt</i> <i>tamasā</i> <i>gūḍham</i> <i>agre</i> [RV.] Au commencement, les ténèbres étaient cachées par les ténèbres.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-guu_dhaartha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>gūḍhārtha</i> [<a href="#entry-artha" class="skt-link">artha</a>] <a href="#entry-guu_dhaartha" class="skt-link">m.</a> phil. sens ésotérique, sens mystique; sens caché — a. <a href="#entry-guu_dhaartha" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-guu_dhaartha" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-guu_dhaarthaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>gūḍhārthā</i> phil. dont le sens est caché.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-guu_dhaarthadiipikaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>gūḍhārthadīpikā</i> [<a href="#entry-diipikaa" class="skt-link">dīpikā</a>] <a href="#entry-guu_dhaarthadiipikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> lit. np. de Gūḍhārthadīpikā, commentaire de la <a href="#entry-Ugiitaa" class="skt-link">Gītā</a> de <a href="#entry-Umadhusuudana_1" class="skt-link">Madhusūdana_1</a> <a href="#entry-Usarasvatii_2" class="skt-link">Sarasvatī_2</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-guu_dha" class="skt-link">gūḍha</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-guu_dhajatru">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>gūḍhajatru</i> [<a href="#entry-jatru" class="skt-link">jatru</a>] a. <a href="#entry-guu_dhajatru" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-guu_dhajatru" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-guu_dhajatru" class="skt-link">f.</a> au cou puissant [«dont le cou est caché (par les muscles)»].
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-gu_nj">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">गुञ्ज्</span> <i>guñj</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. <i>(guñjati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>guñjita</i><i>)</i> bourdonner; glapir.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-gu_nja">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">गुञ्ज</span> <i>guñja</i> [act. <a href="#entry-gu_nj" class="skt-link">guñj</a>] <a href="#entry-gu_nja" class="skt-link">m.</a> bourdonnement <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> natu. bot. <i>Abrus precatorius</i>, liane légumineuse — <a href="#entry-gu_njaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>guñjā</i> natu. son fruit, une graine rouge (ou blanche), utilisée pour des rosaires <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> mesure de poids de joaillerie, qui vaut deux grains [<a href="#entry-yava" class="skt-link">yava</a>] (approx. 0,85 g).
  </div>
</div>
